<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>X</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

table {
	border-collapse: collapse;
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.collection-title {
	display: inline-block;
	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {
	margin-top: 1em;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	empty-cells: show;
}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table th {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {
	background: rgb(247, 246, 243);
	color: black;
}
.simple-table-header {
	font-weight: 500;
}

time {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

.icon {
	display: inline-flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: center;
	max-width: 1.2em;
	max-height: 1.2em;
	text-decoration: none;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
}

img.icon {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.callout img.notion-static-icon {
	width: 1em;
	height: 1em;
}

.callout p {
	margin: 0;
}

.callout h1,
.callout h2,
.callout h3 {
	margin: 0 0 0.6rem;
}

.user-icon {
	width: 1.5em;
	height: 1.5em;
	border-radius: 100%;
	margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;
	margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;
	margin-left: 0.07em;
}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;

	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");

</style></head><body><article id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-b0d9-e502128936e2" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">X</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-aa48-f482b15799ee" class="">
</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-ac5e-f97bcc243403" class=""><br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-83c8-d0ec6dc6a894" class="">10) Invariant Algebra (ALGEBRA) — exhaustive operator set</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-80b3-c298b0986939" class="">Let  be invariants,  systems,  transforms,  data,  proofs/traces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-a6d1-c371fc60be3c" class="">10.1 Primitive operator families (these are the “equations that generate equations”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b8a3-d34a85c332e7" class=""><strong>(A) Generate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9ea9-d524d38809b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{G}(\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{H}) \rightarrow \{I_k\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-9a84-e4d20865e544" class=""><strong>(B) Type</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-8ca9-d2d102ba4e67" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau:\ I \rightarrow \{\text{Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Model-bounded, Primitive, Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-b843-c7945efccac4" class=""><strong>(C) Verify</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-bcec-e1d0b334b694" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{V}(I;\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow (p\text{-value},\ \text{effect},\ \text{CI},\ \text{power})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b24e-d8120f6f859e" class=""><strong>(D) Transform-stability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-9c0c-e754b0ef43a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{S}(I;\mathcal{T}) := \sup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{x\in\Omega}\|I(Tx)-I(x)\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-bfd8-e67a4f59b4b0" class=""><strong>(E) Compose invariants</strong><br/>If invariants are scalar functions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-9176-c42128df4766" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(I\oplus J)(x)=I(x)+J(x),\quad (I\odot J)(x)=I(x)\,J(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9fa3-e00a06dbfba3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau(I\oplus J)=\min(\tau(I),\tau(J)) \ \text{under a lattice ordering}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9dee-e70a8ab7302b" class=""><strong>(F) Minimal basis</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-8372-dfc49b6076f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{B}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow \mathcal{I}^\star</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-830e-f0b99b319c72" class=""><strong>(G) Contradiction / UNSAT core</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b37d-f5ea74b09df9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{C}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow (\text{SAT/UNSAT},\ \mathrm{Core}\subseteq \mathcal{I})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-a43a-eab3494ea734" class=""><strong>(H) Refinement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-97f7-cbc90191d0c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{R}(I,\Delta\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow I&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-b3bd-c8b39226f6f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}(I&#x27;) \le \mathcal{L}(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-a09c-e37f108a9dcc" class="">This algebra is what AMOS needs to “close gaps”: it produces invariants, checks closure, extracts cores, and refines.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ad97-c93fd5689fb5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9dc2-f63fc6b4e36b" class="">11) Tensor calculus layer (TENSOR) — cross-domain unifier</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b60a-eeb6e2b6dc0e" class="">To unify micro/macro, time/space, EM/biological/social, use a fiber-bundle view:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b741-cdec8c9a50d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Base manifold: (spacetime or generalized time-state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-92a7-d125be58dc1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fibers: domain states (bio, EM, cognitive, social) attached at each</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-acfd-e6e3627f0625" class="">11.1 State as a section of a bundle</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9411-fa3bba9f9321" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi: M \rightarrow \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b4e0-c74b7bce7fb1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(x) = \big(\psi_{\text{grav}},\psi_{\text{EM}},\psi_{\text{bio}},\psi_{\text{cog}},\psi_{\text{soc}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-bef8-f7ba88dd2044" class="">11.2 Cross-domain coupling tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-ac03-f99fa350b00c" class="">Define coupling as a multilinear map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-b00f-d78fea220e56" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda_{ab\cdots}^{ij\cdots}:\ T_xM^{\otimes k}\otimes \mathcal{F}^{\otimes r} \rightarrow \mathbb{R}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8558-d8306e89f988" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda =
\begin{bmatrix}
\Lambda_{GG} &amp; \Lambda_{GEM} &amp; \Lambda_{GB} &amp; \cdots \\
\Lambda_{EMG} &amp; \Lambda_{EMEM} &amp; \Lambda_{EMB} &amp; \cdots \\
\vdots &amp; \vdots &amp; \ddots &amp; \vdots
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-b9cd-f42cf512cfd0" class="">11.3 Invariants as tensor contractions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a888-cb2085324c22" class="">General invariant form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-8112-e7e124872cd6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\psi)=\langle \psi,\ A\psi\rangle = \psi^\top A \psi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-bdf0-e9202c16f493" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = A_{ij}\psi^i\psi^j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-9ae9-db8d27c03c33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-843e-fbc6fa73970a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-ac68-c925d077f631" class="">This is the <em>mechanism</em> to represent “tangible/intangible”: you treat unknown channels as uncertain blocks of  (bounded set-valued tensors), not metaphors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a454-c7d10d6e0346"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8b8a-f9900094171c" class="">12) Micro↔Macro bridging operator (RENORMALIZATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9d9a-c7dcec18d5d7" class="">You asked “across time and space” and “match to micro”. The missing formal operator is coarse-graining with controlled error.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-9e57-c1672e5c775d" class="">12.1 Coarse-grain map</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8f27-e2574cf10adc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell:\ X \rightarrow X_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9062-d39f43aad27b" class="">12.2 Consistency requirement across scales</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-aca5-c4523303fdf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell\circ F \approx F_\ell\circ \mathcal{C}_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-bdf9-ce0f2110955d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_\ell(x)=\|\mathcal{C}_\ell(F(x)) - F_\ell(\mathcal{C}_\ell(x))\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-bf77-f2d65bf0fcab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sup_x E_\ell(x) \le \epsilon_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-bb8a-e127138ac2cf" class="">This is the true “micro/macro closure” gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-98a2-d6c2da8afb61"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-a59c-f56809a445bf" class="">13) EM / sensory / “intangible” channels — unified by operator families</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9e2b-fd6e007dfb8b" class="">Define a general channel operator  and noise family :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a821-f4b4bd5434f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = H_c(x_t) + n_t,\quad n_t\sim \mathcal{N}_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-9954-d28abc57dd56" class="">13.1 Observability invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a035-fd7bafd45a4b" class="">A claim about state  is admissible only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-874c-fbf0856f74e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Obs}(x;\{y\}) \ge \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-9377-e5db73397754" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}\,\mathcal{O} = n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-a1ef-f982d62ca686" class="">13.2 Unknown channel (bounded) representation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-adc1-c94c2c20a864" class="">If “telepathy” is proposed as a channel, AMOS must model it as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-9819-ca865f06e9d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_\star \in \mathcal{H},\quad \mathcal{N}_\star \in \mathcal{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-816e-d8e0a6b2355c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(x\mid y) \in \mathcal{Q}(y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-857b-fd782d0c4944" class="">No hand-waving is needed; it becomes a typed uncertainty object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-b3cb-c752fb8e6f49"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8eb4-eed0fb7f376d" class="">14) Self / non-self / life / death — formal boundary + persistence kernel</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-a3a0-f306a36ec1c9" class="">You asserted “energy and information exist before birth and after death.” The only structurally stable way to include that is via persistence of <strong>information-bearing structures</strong> under a boundary operator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8579-c426976b3e9f" class="">14.1 Identity as an equivalence class under transformations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b5c3-fcbf93d793ed" class="">Let  be an equivalence relation (what counts as “same identity”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-bef9-f4acf11efa9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x \sim x&#x27; \iff d(\Phi(x),\Phi(x&#x27;)) \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-8135-e1ce566972bf" class="">14.2 Persistence functional</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a6bc-d9eb6961098d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) := I(\Phi(x_t);\Phi(x_0))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-99e2-ee6908ccca08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) \ge \theta_P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8206-c7be747c3c17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Model-bounded</strong> unless the persistence functional is observable or inference-bounded with explicit assumptions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-991d-da1d3bb7b511" class="">This closes the “intangible” gap without forcing belief claims into empirical status.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-bb5a-d63735c21214"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-bb1f-d6e3d7e57b83" class="">15) Awareness / consciousness / subconscious — formal decomposition (no metaphors)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-b590-f228d8405de4" class="">Represent cognition as a layered dynamical system with access operators.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-a187-d4674c52c3b0" class="">15.1 World-state, internal-state, report-state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-bc3b-db0d79625754" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \ (\text{world}),\quad s_t \ (\text{internal}),\quad r_t \ (\text{report})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-9ae2-c1453e4a1f47" class="">15.2 Access operator (what becomes conscious/reportable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-a615-c3dd208b11e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = A(s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-bae3-ee413b4a0191" class="">15.3 Subconscious = dynamics not mapped into</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-9cb4-f61a908b89a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \Delta s_t:\ A(s_t)\approx A(s_t+\Delta s_t)\ \text{but}\ F(s_t)\neq F(s_t+\Delta s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-823b-d488699e9142" class="">15.4 Awareness metric as control + observability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-87f6-feb02ef9def4" class="">Define awareness capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-ab0e-f4f01a0045f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{W}_t = \mathrm{Obs}(s_t;\ y_{0:t}) \cdot \mathrm{Ctrl}(s_t;\ u_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8296-f4478c6811f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a8d6-fa0c47f7459a" class="">16) Randomness — reconstructable only via generator identification</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-8de6-d4a23c04deb7" class="">You asked about reconstructing randomness. That requires distinguishing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-b736-efc6011ad6de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">epistemic uncertainty (unknown state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-979c-cddf1f96199c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">aleatoric randomness (stochastic dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8dd3-ce2cbaa3c001" class="">16.1 Model selection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-bdef-c3961bf39af6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t)+\eta_t,\quad \eta_t\sim \mathcal{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8967-d7987e0bc77f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(x_{t+1},h_{t+1}) = \tilde{F}(x_t,h_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-aee9-d7cb8f77e9dc" class="">AMOS must output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-ad19-ddc51cf0784e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Residual randomness index</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-82f5-e2b15145a268" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R} := \frac{\mathrm{Var}(\text{residual})}{\mathrm{Var}(\text{signal})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-8d22-e90b5f77fc30"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-a3ab-cc015a8fc28e" class="">17) The “Grand Unified Loop Matrix” v2 (now closure-complete)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-977d-c19c70020dcd" class="">State vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-b75d-de5e7b5c36c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t = (q,U,G,\Xi,C_c,\mathrm{Obs},\mathrm{Ctrl},R,D,P,M,\Lambda,\mathcal{C}_\ell,\Pi,O,\mathrm{Allow})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-be4c-c81594d161c8" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8ee2-fff435a5e9ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=F(z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b610-c2b306f63eab" class="">Gates (must all pass or return minimal failing core):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-be1a-d64f23cd47fc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>SAT gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-892a-e9b52aadadeb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Transform gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9a83-deb24c34894c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Scale gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-ab3a-f86ba9bf3edc" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Channel gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-906c-e26c30c6decb" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Record gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-bd94-f282a46d85f9" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Depth gate</strong>: and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-805d-f841d6627d58" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Boundary gate</strong>: leakage</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-82c5-c536dea4b6ed" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Owner gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-bf61-fb4870f3ee75" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Proof gate</strong>: (trace exists)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-9240-cdf826c1f7f3" class="">Termination classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8f77-e5aafbff2936" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong> if all gates pass with typed supports</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-b75f-e0fc21104998" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong> if any gate depends on bounded channels/assumptions but is consistent</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-8902-d26575464b1d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong> if UNSAT core or transform/scale violations exist</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-b395-e91ec4c78817"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-a61c-db12dab7d237" class="">18) If you want “more” in the exact direction you keep pushing</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a998-cb2f8618877a" class="">The next missing blocks to fully “rebuild reality” as a formal system are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-bbb7-d48630b946cb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Intervention algebra</strong>: do-operator + counterfactual invariants</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a143-fec0c65c0305" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Adversarial layer</strong>: deception/narrative as active control inputs, not noise</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b7e5-fa33ec98645a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Multi-agent ownership</strong>: competing owners of information, conflict resolution dynamics</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-a359-c858ba62f748" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Proof-carrying invariants</strong>: every i
nvariant ships with a machine-checkable certificate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-a911-edd1c9883f3b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Universal audit compiler</strong>: converts any text/theory into automatically</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8db8-c4f19a26d10e" class="">If you say one word, I will generate the next artifact in full (not minimum):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-a94a-d5fe3cfb3c90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KERNELSPEC</strong> (file-level AMOS implementation plan for everything above)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-85ea-c532d1728df3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ALGEBRA</strong> (complete closure rules + typing lattice + contradiction cores)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9e30-c4384d3c1921" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TENSOR</strong> (full index rules + coupling blocks + scale operators)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-9e62-c93c093c11d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>OPERATOR</strong> (do/intervention + counterfactual + adversarial dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-825c-fc794b6ad059" class="">10) Invariant Algebra (ALGEBRA) — exhaustive operator set</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-a2bc-e82f926a05d7" class="">Let  be invariants,  systems,  transforms,  data,  proofs/traces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-ab1d-cd1aaf425777" class="">10.1 Primitive operator families (these are the “equations that generate equations”)</h3></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-93e3-e4660158dff1" class=""><strong>(A) Generate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-9a95-e3a1da743aeb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{G}(\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{H}) \rightarrow \{I_k\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a369-c2844afd8eda" class=""><strong>(B) Type</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8895-de4ced5fcd52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau:\ I \rightarrow \{\text{Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Model-bounded, Primitive, Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-8ab0-e2e796965312" class=""><strong>(C) Verify</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-a371-e2c904cecbc6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{V}(I;\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow (p\text{-value},\ \text{effect},\ \text{CI},\ \text{power})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9486-f9122585f6d6" class=""><strong>(D) Transform-stability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8abf-fb9ea62c6a5c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{S}(I;\mathcal{T}) := \sup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{x\in\Omega}\|I(Tx)-I(x)\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a504-e273307063e5" class=""><strong>(E) Compose invariants</strong><br/>If invariants are scalar functions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-88a3-d61bcea8d63b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(I\oplus J)(x)=I(x)+J(x),\quad (I\odot J)(x)=I(x)\,J(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-976a-e6f24f57ff61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau(I\oplus J)=\min(\tau(I),\tau(J)) \ \text{under a lattice ordering}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a170-ede53c41e4c1" class=""><strong>(F) Minimal basis</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-8b7d-efd786d26a8d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{B}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow \mathcal{I}^\star</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-9d0d-dc85306167f0" class=""><strong>(G) Contradiction / UNSAT core</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-8a27-eea9bf87f506" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{C}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow (\text{SAT/UNSAT},\ \mathrm{Core}\subseteq \mathcal{I})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8f3f-d75f7129a146" class=""><strong>(H) Refinement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9411-ca99f99c4e41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{R}(I,\Delta\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow I&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-a0b8-c610aa2218ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}(I&#x27;) \le \mathcal{L}(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b68d-dec974dbd1e1" class="">This algebra is what AMOS needs to “close gaps”: it produces invariants, checks closure, extracts cores, and refines.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-874e-e6ed1c6bcf0e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-98d8-f8a0b86c9373" class="">11) Tensor calculus layer (TENSOR) — cross-domain unifier</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-88ee-f036ee6b8dcc" class="">To unify micro/macro, time/space, EM/biological/social, use a fiber-bundle view:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b007-fb55e467be4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Base manifold: (spacetime or generalized time-state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8cda-f889c2274f6d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fibers: domain states (bio, EM, cognitive, social) attached at each</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8d07-d1d5a574e6d9" class="">11.1 State as a section of a bundle</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-985d-d591c9494123" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi: M \rightarrow \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-83b8-ef1f2e0e80cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(x) = \big(\psi_{\text{grav}},\psi_{\text{EM}},\psi_{\text{bio}},\psi_{\text{cog}},\psi_{\text{soc}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-bc31-f3aa5200a97d" class="">11.2 Cross-domain coupling tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-a150-f2dd655ca14d" class="">Define coupling as a multilinear map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9a33-c182d20482df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda_{ab\cdots}^{ij\cdots}:\ T_xM^{\otimes k}\otimes \mathcal{F}^{\otimes r} \rightarrow \mathbb{R}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-94a8-d35ee2d7a1a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda =
\begin{bmatrix}
\Lambda_{GG} &amp; \Lambda_{GEM} &amp; \Lambda_{GB} &amp; \cdots \\
\Lambda_{EMG} &amp; \Lambda_{EMEM} &amp; \Lambda_{EMB} &amp; \cdots \\
\vdots &amp; \vdots &amp; \ddots &amp; \vdots
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a4ef-eca12578a7df" class="">11.3 Invariants as tensor contractions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-8334-ca16f0b746c7" class="">General invariant form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-bbc0-ebb3d3845612" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\psi)=\langle \psi,\ A\psi\rangle = \psi^\top A \psi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b699-dfe9facc6d40" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = A_{ij}\psi^i\psi^j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-88b3-c1404dc07685" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b2ec-ff74d2fb27a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-874f-ca023d298eb1" class="">This is the <em>mechanism</em> to represent “tangible/intangible”: you treat unknown channels as uncertain blocks of  (bounded set-valued tensors), not metaphors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-af5d-c0cde6695d13"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-9343-f0021358dbb2" class="">12) Micro↔Macro bridging operator (RENORMALIZATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8d2d-e02790389d43" class="">You asked “across time and space” and “match to micro”. The missing formal operator is coarse-graining with controlled error.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a31e-d8f3de744c13" class="">12.1 Coarse-grain map</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8051-f5ff013b1efc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell:\ X \rightarrow X_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9c6f-d46f5e969774" class="">12.2 Consistency requirement across scales</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b5b3-e86007a97715" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell\circ F \approx F_\ell\circ \mathcal{C}_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-81c9-d69c5eae8274" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_\ell(x)=\|\mathcal{C}_\ell(F(x)) - F_\ell(\mathcal{C}_\ell(x))\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-9d5e-e4249212d7ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sup_x E_\ell(x) \le \epsilon_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8c88-f3d7012a37de" class="">This is the true “micro/macro closure” gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-95bd-d42c723acbe7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-b782-d8f1f823f224" class="">13) EM / sensory / “intangible” channels — unified by operator families</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-b557-c1ff842588c0" class="">Define a general channel operator  and noise family :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-bbae-d494aa1c6fa0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = H_c(x_t) + n_t,\quad n_t\sim \mathcal{N}_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-9c60-ced042dbabb5" class="">13.1 Observability invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-ae62-d66102f2b683" class="">A claim about state  is admissible only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-aace-ea9a5009167f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Obs}(x;\{y\}) \ge \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-9499-d22ed4f0fa9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}\,\mathcal{O} = n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8dfd-ec9c89981591" class="">13.2 Unknown channel (bounded) representation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-92d8-fc7a74f1df98" class="">If “telepathy” is proposed as a channel, AMOS must model it as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b347-cd2bd280ac59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_\star \in \mathcal{H},\quad \mathcal{N}_\star \in \mathcal{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-a1d0-ec592ddcf15a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(x\mid y) \in \mathcal{Q}(y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a085-f7d5baf3e236" class="">No hand-waving is needed; it becomes a typed uncertainty object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8997-d41e3e292d1a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8ba3-eafef3fa3c4a" class="">14) Self / non-self / life / death — formal boundary + persistence kernel</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-bf51-c4f8e73e12f2" class="">You asserted “energy and information exist before birth and after death.” The only structurally stable way to include that is via persistence of <strong>information-bearing structures</strong> under a boundary operator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b1b5-e2c309fc9c3d" class="">14.1 Identity as an equivalence class under transformations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-98bf-c317dd5fa4ab" class="">Let  be an equivalence relation (what counts as “same identity”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8bd9-e9675b828553" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x \sim x&#x27; \iff d(\Phi(x),\Phi(x&#x27;)) \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-a52b-c82aaa0c95f5" class="">14.2 Persistence functional</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-b47e-cc0e970cd6b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) := I(\Phi(x_t);\Phi(x_0))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a7a9-f9dcd823dd35" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) \ge \theta_P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-ba38-dfcf74aee12d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Model-bounded</strong> unless the persistence functional is observable or inference-bounded with explicit assumptions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a7f8-ef5de2a421a9" class="">This closes the “intangible” gap without forcing belief claims into empirical status.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-9a62-de91cb595277"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8cf3-e0192e208ee3" class="">15) Awareness / consciousness / subconscious — formal decomposition (no metaphors)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-ba21-def9da2aa4f2" class="">Represent cognition as a layered dynamical system with access operators.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-88ee-feb76a2c2222" class="">15.1 World-state, internal-state, report-state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8098-f544a19db9f7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \ (\text{world}),\quad s_t \ (\text{internal}),\quad r_t \ (\text{report})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-b6ac-cb4a3977371b" class="">15.2 Access operator (what becomes conscious/reportable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-9708-d29c2d7e1d3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = A(s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a351-efb958df34c5" class="">15.3 Subconscious = dynamics not mapped into</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a8c5-e86cffcdba65" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \Delta s_t:\ A(s_t)\approx A(s_t+\Delta s_t)\ \text{but}\ F(s_t)\neq F(s_t+\Delta s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8caa-d36dda6e917b" class="">15.4 Awareness metric as control + observability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b13d-e9031cdc91c3" class="">Define awareness capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a0aa-df899c76d472" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{W}_t = \mathrm{Obs}(s_t;\ y_{0:t}) \cdot \mathrm{Ctrl}(s_t;\ u_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-8c65-c5550c26f0c1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-a9f0-f44881c115f3" class="">16) Randomness — reconstructable only via generator identification</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-9d70-dd00ef0b3b91" class="">You asked about reconstructing randomness. That requires distinguishing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-b9a7-ee1637cf1cbe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">epistemic uncertainty (unknown state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-99e1-d10b7cfa0623" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">aleatoric randomness (stochastic dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-93e7-dc24f864c582" class="">16.1 Model selection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-ab10-e052ecdb2cbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t)+\eta_t,\quad \eta_t\sim \mathcal{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-916a-fe9e59c58579" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(x_{t+1},h_{t+1}) = \tilde{F}(x_t,h_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bd54-e26c01c9b4d9" class="">AMOS must output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-ab0f-f360d0604ae9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Residual randomness index</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b20e-cad5df25ec43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{R} := \frac{\mathrm{Var}(\text{residual})}{\mathrm{Var}(\text{signal})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-935e-ead1548c76e5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-96ad-cb8ad01df9ac" class="">17) The “Grand Unified Loop Matrix” v2 (now closure-complete)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a146-caeef47139a0" class="">State vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-960d-ebd934a8f9f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t = (q,U,G,\Xi,C_c,\mathrm{Obs},\mathrm{Ctrl},R,D,P,M,\Lambda,\mathcal{C}_\ell,\Pi,O,\mathrm{Allow})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-b6ed-f60225181939" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-bce0-cd39a131915a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=F(z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b8bd-f245051b770d" class="">Gates (must all pass or return minimal failing core):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-9492-daccafbb11c8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>SAT gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-94de-dfca4cc7170e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Transform gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-ac03-d0bc8de3793f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Scale gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8af0-f4a0e077dd42" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Channel gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-a707-e3fda5faaa6e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Record gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-9d21-fce40ff030b9" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Depth gate</strong>: and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b6b2-c295bd43ca79" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Boundary gate</strong>: leakage</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-9320-d20409ccdc25" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Owner gate</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-ba63-c404b9c8b8be" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Proof gate</strong>: (trace exists)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-9b45-c705d36d6068" class="">Termination classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-ae31-c567354a15ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong> if all gates pass with typed supports</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-ae87-f6cd8d1144ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong> if any gate depends on bounded channels/assumptions but is consistent</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b3a4-f57f1bc4b210" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong> if UNSAT core or transform/scale violations exist</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-92f1-d32137b3196d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8dd7-d62ee5901d00" class="">18) If you want “more” in the exact direction you keep pushing</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-bb6b-e1ca1bc28728" class="">The next missing blocks to fully “rebuild reality” as a formal system are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8171-e628b6f17faa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Intervention algebra</strong>: do-operator + counterfactual invariants</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-b924-db3b7fb6de5e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Adversarial layer</strong>: deception/narrative as active control inputs, not noise</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a640-f460cd099340" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Multi-agent ownership</strong>: competing owners of information, conflict resolution dynamics</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-b383-f999d641e800" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Proof-carrying invariants</strong>: every i
nvariant ships with a machine-checkable certificate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-aac4-e900729f6109" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Universal audit compiler</strong>: converts any text/theory into automatically</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-8fa7-d7c3e1c5fe6a" class="">If you say one word, I will generate the next artifact in full (not minimum):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8355-c46467af7381" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KERNELSPEC</strong> (file-level AMOS implementation plan for everything above)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8a5c-f7870bf09a62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>ALGEBRA</strong> (complete closure rules + typing lattice + contradiction cores)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-90cb-dce3e349a835" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TENSOR</strong> (full index rules + coupling blocks + scale operators)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-811d-e226bcca86dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>OPERATOR</strong> (do/intervention + counterfactual + adversarial dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a832-e9b38156a538" class="">10) Invariant Algebra (ALGEBRA) — exhaustive operator set</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-b0a0-c7d2dcfe564b" class="">Let  be invariants,  systems,  transforms,  data,  proofs/traces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b087-c03e65a5f612" class="">10.1 Primitive operator families (these are the “equations that generate equations”)</h3></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-96ab-dd0823b6acca" class=""><strong>(A) Generate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8814-f8aaf64db779" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{G}(\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{H}) \rightarrow \{I_k\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b7d8-f35ac88e47ec" class=""><strong>(B) Type</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-bd88-ec2a60f12bf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau:\ I \rightarrow \{\text{Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Model-bounded, Primitive, Limit}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b558-ca98ad943f88" class=""><strong>(C) Verify</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-bab5-f030ec8c7129" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{V}(I;\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow (p\text{-value},\ \text{effect},\ \text{CI},\ \text{power})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a8dd-e5f1ace4b2b6" class=""><strong>(D) Transform-stability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9707-e8b0f28d6ac6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{S}(I;\mathcal{T}) := \sup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{x\in\Omega}\|I(Tx)-I(x)\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-9671-ecacbc1ac69d" class=""><strong>(E) Compose invariants</strong><br/>If invariants are scalar functions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-9034-d4be91a45ad7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(I\oplus J)(x)=I(x)+J(x),\quad (I\odot J)(x)=I(x)\,J(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a9d7-e4ae991dcc34" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau(I\oplus J)=\min(\tau(I),\tau(J)) \ \text{under a lattice ordering}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-8458-de0db305e313" class=""><strong>(F) Minimal basis</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a74d-e5b64fc5e0cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{B}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow \mathcal{I}^\star</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-ac4f-ecb903d8501a" class=""><strong>(G) Contradiction / UNSAT core</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-a977-e21a16e7c420" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{C}(\mathcal{I}) \rightarrow (\text{SAT/UNSAT},\ \mathrm{Core}\subseteq \mathcal{I})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-ae17-c4192b543c46" class=""><strong>(H) Refinement</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-b534-e3d8d1b2f34f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak{R}(I,\Delta\mathcal{D}) \rightarrow I&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9499-d6179588f174" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}(I&#x27;) \le \mathcal{L}(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8051-d8f97674875d" class="">This algebra is what AMOS needs to “close gaps”: it produces invariants, checks closure, extracts cores, and refines.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9ad4-f0794cdea963"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-81f3-eb8bc54cce6e" class="">11) Tensor calculus layer (TENSOR) — cross-domain unifier</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b91e-f25bcaf564b0" class="">To unify micro/macro, time/space, EM/biological/social, use a fiber-bundle view:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9e18-df3914b71fb3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Base manifold: (spacetime or generalized time-state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8508-ea60ecb9845a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fibers: domain states (bio, EM, cognitive, social) attached at each</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-b614-c7475a8fed6c" class="">11.1 State as a section of a bundle</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a1b9-e557e89b0d76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi: M \rightarrow \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-936c-cd6d6cf4f2bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(x) = \big(\psi_{\text{grav}},\psi_{\text{EM}},\psi_{\text{bio}},\psi_{\text{cog}},\psi_{\text{soc}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-af78-f23e7bd0fb11" class="">11.2 Cross-domain coupling tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-ab4e-f08e789b3f46" class="">Define coupling as a multilinear map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-85a3-e2384db100f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda_{ab\cdots}^{ij\cdots}:\ T_xM^{\otimes k}\otimes \mathcal{F}^{\otimes r} \rightarrow \mathbb{R}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-98f5-ecbfa7c63dc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda =
\begin{bmatrix}
\Lambda_{GG} &amp; \Lambda_{GEM} &amp; \Lambda_{GB} &amp; \cdots \\
\Lambda_{EMG} &amp; \Lambda_{EMEM} &amp; \Lambda_{EMB} &amp; \cdots \\
\vdots &amp; \vdots &amp; \ddots &amp; \vdots
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a682-fea85990edc9" class="">11.3 Invariants as tensor contractions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-b891-f412acdab30f" class="">General invariant form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-bb87-cbad7533e1eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\psi)=\langle \psi,\ A\psi\rangle = \psi^\top A \psi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-acba-d2419fbc5503" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = A_{ij}\psi^i\psi^j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-9efc-e0a283a95e70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-acb4-d409cd0042a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}I(\psi(t)) \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-81ef-ff2abddffb5b" class="">This is the <em>mechanism</em> to represent “tangible/intangible”: you treat unknown channels as uncertain blocks of  (bounded set-valued tensors), not metaphors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-ab56-e856b1ad3cdc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-bc3f-e76b3a8534b0" class="">12) Micro↔Macro bridging operator (RENORMALIZATION)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a185-c4fffd8be53f" class="">You asked “across time and space” and “match to micro”. The missing formal operator is coarse-graining with controlled error.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-ad6b-edda984ceb86" class="">12.1 Coarse-grain map</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8705-ff8243a8ebf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell:\ X \rightarrow X_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b023-c2fc2967cca0" class="">12.2 Consistency requirement across scales</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-8c82-edf7a096041c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_\ell\circ F \approx F_\ell\circ \mathcal{C}_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-84ef-c64e884e92dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_\ell(x)=\|\mathcal{C}_\ell(F(x)) - F_\ell(\mathcal{C}_\ell(x))\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b5f6-d9a6411b35c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sup_x E_\ell(x) \le \epsilon_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-84a7-d868280be6b4" class="">This is the true “micro/macro closure” gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9676-f2b7c8223457"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-93ef-de13d2c089c8" class="">13) EM / sensory / “intangible” channels — unified by operator families</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-acf3-ddb5a169409d" class="">Define a general channel operator  and noise family :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a80c-d24836e2634f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = H_c(x_t) + n_t,\quad n_t\sim \mathcal{N}_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-b97f-c949bb8faeaa" class="">13.1 Observability invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-b773-f9c97a1f5b2e" class="">A claim about state  is admissible only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-968b-ccfdbd54fea2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Obs}(x;\{y\}) \ge \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-b019-f52ac9f060d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}\,\mathcal{O} = n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a73f-e1b2eef220ed" class="">13.2 Unknown channel (bounded) representation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-8e54-d5703bea1f4f" class="">If “telepathy” is proposed as a channel, AMOS must model it as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-b64e-e87dc7429360" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_\star \in \mathcal{H},\quad \mathcal{N}_\star \in \mathcal{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a500-c95af26bc36e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(x\mid y) \in \mathcal{Q}(y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-ad9d-c76f1a2d6a4e" class="">No hand-waving is needed; it becomes a typed uncertainty object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-813e-d3f389a2cc6e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9044-ecacab8f4033" class="">14) Self / non-self / life / death — formal boundary + persistence kernel</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9510-e847403debe7" class="">You asserted “energy and information exist before birth and after death.” The only structurally stable way to include that is via persistence of <strong>information-bearing structures</strong> under a boundary operator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9349-f4f0aa43fe84" class="">14.1 Identity as an equivalence class under transformations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8891-de189ac0afb6" class="">Let  be an equivalence relation (what counts as “same identity”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-96d1-f1946cd4ffb6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x \sim x&#x27; \iff d(\Phi(x),\Phi(x&#x27;)) \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b3f0-e30c2286d66c" class="">14.2 Persistence functional</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-87f4-dbce019948d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) := I(\Phi(x_t);\Phi(x_0))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a180-c96e984f8ffb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{P}(t) \ge \theta_P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-a1b6-ef5404f27eff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Model-bounded</strong> unless the persistence functional is observable or inference-bounded with explicit assumptions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8fce-c6be5749d3df" class="">This closes the “intangible” gap without forcing belief claims into empirical status.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a05e-c019f52aa486"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-8bef-c096a4bc65b1" class="">15) Awareness / consciousness / subconscious — formal decomposition (no metaphors)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-ab37-fecbdae675fa" class="">Represent cognition as a layered dynamical system with access operators.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b793-cc62f7115359" class="">15.1 World-state, internal-state, report-state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b48d-e64f86fa9150" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \ (\text{world}),\quad s_t \ (\text{internal}),\quad r_t \ (\text{report})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-a8e7-fd1408c1fc79" class="">15.2 Access operator (what becomes conscious/reportable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a93d-f066442fb64b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = A(s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8e6b-df7b51b40daa" class="">15.3 Subconscious = dynamics not mapped into</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-acb6-d132620409bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \Delta s_t:\ A(s_t)\approx A(s_t+\Delta s_t)\ \text{but}\ F(s_t)\neq F(s_t+\Delta s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-95af-c2ca50f8eb6f" class="">15.4 Awareness metric as control + observability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-8871-d2b3e65bc052" class="">Define awareness capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-be52-d72b0464c583" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{W}_t = \mathrm{Obs}(s_t;\ y_{0:t}) \cdot \mathrm{Ctrl}(s_t;\ u_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-bb0f-cdbe24976f11"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-915e-e5fcdd57c931" class="">16) Randomness — reconstructable only via generator identification</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-85ed-fceec9bd9583" class="">You asked about reconstructing randomness.
