\Delta R_t \le C_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-aa97-cd54702ab31a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-8a6e-c30588e213d9" class="">B3) Cross-species developmental staging</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a063-d9b1cd93c99f" class="">Cross-species invariance requires developmental normalization and sensorimotor differences.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-b7e9-d8626752fdd7" class=""><strong>Must exist</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-bd00-e7fa83822446" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/species/development.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b591-ff0acd471bdd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>08_WORLD_MODEL/models/species/allometry.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-907f-d5f8613d782e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a9dc-ebb5359e3358" class="">B4) Identity: self vs non-self boundary operator (IMMUNE)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-8d05-d3729926ae38" class="">If you want “self and non-self”, you need a boundary operator, not philosophy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-ab95-f9c6182b7401" class="">Define boundary  that partitions degrees of freedom:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-acb7-f12eed920834" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{H} = \mathcal{H}_{self}\otimes \mathcal{H}_{env}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9e6e-f1da0086f0ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \mathrm{Leakage}(self\to env) \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8723-e051d5870702"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-bbdc-ef805c7cd26c" class="">C) The “missing matrix blocks” (what your Grand Unified Matrix still lacks)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Observability</strong> (what can be known)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b7de-e88aac0e4680" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Adversary</strong> (active overwrite)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8a3d-d057e58d67b0" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-a434-e0bd50200eac" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Uncertainty s
et</strong> (intangible boundedness)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-bf3c-e7526996ac31" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Proof trace</strong> (why termination holds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-80c0-f2e98167b49a" class="">So the expanded state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-aab8-f9827be73b98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t=(q,U,G,\Xi,R,D,P,M,K,\ S,\ O,A,C,L,B,\mathcal{P},\Pi)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-9d7c-f1bcd870916c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b71c-c8f6aae30fe9" class="">D) What AMOS should build next (to eliminate these gaps)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-9bf3-ffa1294207bd" class="">If the directive is “NO QUESTIONS, execute”, the next build step is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8448-c84afa41b428" class="">Step 1 — Implement SSOT registries + transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-bda8-ce633370e86f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">variable registry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b232-d4193ea2e853" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">units + domains + support types</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-8fcb-db7a3dae6329" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transform group library + closure tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-ac34-efdad44fecc3" class="">Step 2 — Implement contradiction engine + proof artifacts</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-ad34-f40fb51841ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">satisfiable check</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9244-da49ed9951c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">minimal unsat cores</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-97e5-cf0c58313de5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">decision trace outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-87b2-fd172e2e3a3a" class="">Step 3 — Implement observability map + uncertainty channels</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-9f34-c35e7fc8af6f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observable/inferable/unobservable classification</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a62f-c527dab1a6ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bounded channels (intervals / sets of distributions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-a811-cf4d8874ff0d" class="">Step 4 — Upgrade SimEngine with missing blocks</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b0cd-e3828f45f58d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adversarial overwrite</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-9ed9-e19041dce419" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a4c8-db753df5f355" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">causal space-time locality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a885-d7ca0c615245" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">boundary operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-82a2-c6b4fac0d807"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b407-f9cacf1fc8da" class="">E) Single “MAX POWER” prompt to add these missing components (copy/paste)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-99f9-d58524c9e143" class=""><strong>PROMPT</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-9691-df903e198cc9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Role: AMOS Kernel Expansion Engine (SSOT c
ompliant)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-a051-d712a728cf1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Goal: Add missing closure components to DSL+Tensor+SimEngine so termination can be Valid/Bounded/Invalid with proofs.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-aa48-d2e30e2735e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constraints: Python 3.9, offline, deterministic, no destructive moves, stage-copy only.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-996f-c79e6e673003" class=""><strong>Execute</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9e4e-fa94b5416d40" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Create SSOT registries:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-a3f3-e4d1a5ceabd6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">variable_registry/registry.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-b009-d707cd7130da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">canonical_definitions/{units,domains,support_types,species_codebook}.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-96b6-d78826aabdb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic id scheme for variables and invariants</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-b14c-e0f4e1da18d9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Build Transform Group Library:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-8435-de0dff45b70e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">group axioms (identity, composition, inverse/none)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-a4ae-efbced2f1ba8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">catalog of t
ransforms (time-shift, scale, coarsegrain, species-lift, basis)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9e53-dc51dff3b1f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">closure tests</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-8183-c425b2b16142" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Build Contradiction Engine (03_IMMUNE):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-ac7a-e60c633838b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant predicate evaluation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-aa8f-c3009f5d8b19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bounded solver (finite domains)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b6de-f53f7e97ede8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">minimal unsat core extractor</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9531-e197667bb4b0" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Build Observability + Uncertainty:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-88a2-fd70abb96ea8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">map variables to sensors/parsers/adapters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-92e9-dd915627cb1c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">classify variables as Observable/Inferable/Unobservable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-9fbc-fb156403f165" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">implement bounded channels using interval sets or distribution families</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a7dd-dc2becc9c33d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Upgrade SimEngine with m
issing state blocks:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-acb6-f9dbb345f995" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add A_t adversarial overwrite</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-b75e-e59869235978" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add C_t EM/media capacity and enforce ΔR_t ≤ C_t</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-9877-f3ce2574478f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add L causal locality constraints on state transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-b835-c42db0308d48" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">add B self/non-self boundary operator using information flow</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-8706-ee42d295cd60" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Law Engine proof artifacts:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9bad-f505545f81a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant_proofs.jsonl (what was checked + results)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-bde5-d9167c1e8521" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">decision_trace.json (why termination classification)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8874-c117bb1b771f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">unsat_cores.jsonl when contradictions occur</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-921a-dc611b01d491" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Tests:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a0ad-f76c2db897bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic outputs (hash-stable)</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-ac7e-dfdbe1dd17cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transform invariance tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9d8f-f575196751c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradiction detection tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a970-db541ee68378" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observability classification tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-9741-fd8db9bed775" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sim regression golden tests</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-827e-d9eadb030d47" class=""><strong>Terminate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9544-c6e3b5da5243" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Valid only if:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9da1-d8b02d2373db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">transforms are closed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b295-ce59302e6be5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">contradictions are detected with unsat cores</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b1d4-e1657624b62f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">every invariant has support type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-a2ea-dd51891a7199" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">every variable has observability status</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8062-ef1ab1040615" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">sim engine enforces c
apacity/locality/boundary gates<br/>Otherwise Bounded/Invalid with explicit reasons.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8baa-ce6a94291f09"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-8751-e4dee792dacd" class="">If you want the <em>next</em> output from me in this thread, say one word:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8f39-d17519aa314c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>REGISTRY</strong> (I output the exact JSON schemas + ids + required keys)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8203-d356bbbdf7c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CONTRADICTION</strong> (I output the solver spec + minimal unsat core algorithm + tests)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-bff3-ec71eb679316" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>OBSERVABILITY</strong> (I output the full mapping spec + classifications + equations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9144-ff2ea511ed67" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SIM-UPGRADE</strong> (I output the expanded state equations + gates + scenario suite)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b73e-f584e40de608" class="">1) DSL — Full Invariant DSL (Grammar + Semantics + Artifacts)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-8947-cb04daadf812" class="">1.1 Canonical files (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-b8d6-e4f578a5ca23" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/grammar.ebnf</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-abcf-fbd3d7204e66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/ast.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-87aa-d3f4a4669a70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/parser.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-a267-daa6e62ac311" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/typecheck.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-bd3b-cf9c82268e0d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/eval.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-ad72-d791dfb42d7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/stdlib.dsl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-8933-de3372fe9a70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/examples/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-aeeb-c6b5c719450c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/validators/dsl_tests.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-872d-f779ac94672f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9f6a-ecfee7856240" class="">1.2 Core design constraints</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-9522-ede1a2c66fcc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic parsing + evaluation (no wall-clock, no r
andomness).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-91db-e3f849963147" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fully typed using registry types + units.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-bd4f-d9114846fb2c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Supports: equality, inequality, monotone, threshold/phase, conservation, closure, gates, transforms.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9702-d145d0c991ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Explicit scope: domains/layers/species/time/space.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b136-d4e7f3a388f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Explicit support typing (Empirical/Inferential/Definitional/Model-bounded/Primitive/Limit).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-ac43-f218d6ad4dd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b730-eb0172365bd2" class="">1.3 Grammar (EBNF)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-a704-fc9e782a6501" class=""><strong>grammar.ebnf</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-9b9e-fab16aa5bc73" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">program      := { stmt } ;

stmt         := def_stmt | inv_stmt | gate_stmt | transform_stmt | assert_stmt | scope_stmt ;

scope_stmt   := &quot;scope&quot; &quot;{&quot; scope_kv { &quot;,&quot; scope_kv } &quot;}&quot; ;
scope_kv     := key &quot;:&quot; value ;

def_stmt     := &quot;def&quot; ident &quot;:&quot; type &quot;=&quot; expr &quot;;&quot; ;
type         := &quot;scalar&quot; | &quot;vector&quot; &quot;[&quot; int &quot;]&quot;
              | &quot;matrix&quot; &quot;[&quot; int &quot;,&quot; int &quot;]&quot;
              | &quot;tensor&quot; &quot;[&quot; dims &quot;]&quot;
              | &quot;bool&quot; | &quot;int&quot; | &quot;float&quot;
              | &quot;units&quot; &quot;&lt;&quot; unit_expr &quot;&gt;&quot; ;

inv_stmt     := &quot;invariant&quot; ident inv_meta &quot;{&quot; inv_body &quot;}&quot; ;
inv_meta     := &quot;(&quot; { meta_kv { &quot;,&quot; meta_kv } } &quot;)&quot; ;
meta_kv      := key &quot;:&quot; value ;

inv_body     := &quot;let&quot; { def_stmt } &quot;require&quot; predicate &quot;;&quot; { &quot;test&quot; test_spec &quot;;&quot; } ;

gate_stmt    := &quot;gate&quot; ident gate_meta &quot;{&quot; gate_body &quot;}&quot; ;
gate_meta    := &quot;(&quot; { meta_kv { &quot;,&quot; meta_kv } } &quot;)&quot; ;
gate_body    := &quot;if&quot; predicate &quot;then&quot; verdict &quot;else&quot; verdict &quot;;&quot; ;

transform_stmt := &quot;transform&quot; ident trans_meta &quot;{&quot; trans_body &quot;}&quot; ;
trans_meta   := &quot;(&quot; { meta_kv { &quot;,&quot; meta_kv } } &quot;)&quot; ;
trans_body   := &quot;apply&quot; &quot;:&quot; ident &quot;;&quot; &quot;inverse&quot; &quot;:&quot; (ident | &quot;none&quot;) &quot;;&quot; ;

assert_stmt  := &quot;assert&quot; predicate &quot;;&quot; ;

predicate    := expr relop expr
              | &quot;forall&quot; binders &quot;:&quot; predicate
              | &quot;exists&quot; binders &quot;:&quot; predicate
              | predicate (&quot;and&quot; | &quot;or&quot;) predicate
              | &quot;not&quot; predicate
              | &quot;(&quot; predicate &quot;)&quot; ;

binders      := binder { &quot;,&quot; binder } ;
binder       := ident &quot;in&quot; set_expr ;

expr         := term { (&quot;+&quot;|&quot;-&quot;) term } ;
term         := factor { (&quot;*&quot;|&quot;/&quot;) factor } ;
factor       := atom | (&quot;-&quot; factor) | funcall | sum_expr | diff_expr ;
atom         := number | ident | varref | &quot;(&quot; expr &quot;)&quot; ;

varref       := &quot;v&quot; &quot;(&quot; string &quot;)&quot; [ indexer ] ;
indexer      := &quot;[&quot; expr { &quot;,&quot; expr } &quot;]&quot; ;

funcall      := ident &quot;(&quot; [ expr { &quot;,&quot; expr } ] &quot;)&quot; ;

sum_expr     := &quot;sum&quot; &quot;(&quot; binder &quot;:&quot; expr &quot;)&quot; ;
diff_expr    := &quot;delta&quot; &quot;(&quot; expr &quot;,&quot; &quot;t&quot; &quot;)&quot; ;

relop        := &quot;==&quot; | &quot;!=&quot; | &quot;&lt;=&quot; | &quot;&gt;=&quot; | &quot;&lt;&quot; | &quot;&gt;&quot; ;

test_spec    := &quot;under&quot; ident &quot;with&quot; &quot;cases&quot; int ;

verdict      := &quot;PASS&quot; | &quot;FAIL&quot; | &quot;BOUNDED&quot; ;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8539-ec8c1c52e91d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8689-d1b0eaa38150" class="">1.4 Semantics (how it executes)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-ade2-cb892f31790a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>v(&quot;...&quot;)</code> resolves a registry variable id (must exist in <code>variable_registry/registry.json</code>).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-ae47-f644da0edd36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Units enforced at typecheck:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-9008-f687a33189db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">addition/subtraction require equal dimensions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-adec-c4ab910acd2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">multiplication/division compose dimensions</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a316-db153e8e5af3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>delta(x,t)</code> is discrete-time difference: (time index must exist).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-ac87-cf89a36336f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Quantifiers (<code>forall/exists</code>) are bounded to explicit set expressions (no infinite quantification).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-92b9-de18f74aa81e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>test under T with cases N</code> runs transform survival tests using deterministic case generation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-bd00-cbd0ffe12020"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-9218-e01078230f3b" class="">1.5 Standard library (required primitives)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-837c-fbde5f54df17" class=""><strong>stdlib.dsl (conceptual)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b2a2-e8e5db1f8a45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>H(p)</code> entropy functional for discrete distributions (model-bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-90e6-c606289abf0c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>I(a,b)</code> mutual information (model-bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-839c-cd3f7a400e5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Klen(x)</code> description-length proxy (AST length + compressed byte length)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-8730-e8f2c0ea7d93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Cap(P,T,Bdot)</code> Landauer power bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-96e1-da1027c6d9e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Imax(E,R)</code> Bekenstein bits bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a3af-f351ebc88ecf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Ih(H)</code> horizon bits bound (de Sitter proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-8b22-e95ad157cd69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CodeOK(p, pth)</code> coding threshold predicate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-909a-edd8449a61bb" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>Stable(alpha, tau, rho)</code> delay-stability predicate (control gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-bc24-cacc9b0c728f" class="">All functions must declare support type:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a82f-c01e5d6000d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if it relies on uncomputable objects (true Kolmogorov), it must be replaced by length proxy (<code>Klen</code>) and marked Model-bounded.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-8b27-f8f1001c5a45"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-82a5-cb0c1dc60fcf" class="">1.6 Example: Record monotonicity + budgets as an invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-86a3-f24d1e5c07e6" class=""><strong>examples/records_budget.dsl</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-93a2-dde20be8a079" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">scope { domain:&quot;cosmo+info+bio&quot;, layer:&quot;micro_to_macro&quot;, species:&quot;all&quot; }

def R : scalar = v(&quot;v.records.redundancy&quot;)[t] ;
def G : scalar = v(&quot;v.energy.gradient&quot;)[t] ;
def Xi: scalar = v(&quot;v.noise.rate&quot;)[t] ;
def P : scalar = v(&quot;v.power.available&quot;)[t] ;
def T : scalar = v(&quot;v.temp&quot;)[t] ;
def Bdot : scalar = v(&quot;v.bits.erase_rate&quot;)[t] ;
def Irec : scalar = v(&quot;v.info.records&quot;)[t] ;
def Imod : scalar = v(&quot;v.info.models&quot;)[t] ;
def Imax : scalar = v(&quot;v.info.max&quot;)[t] ;

invariant I_records_budget (support:&quot;Model-bounded&quot;, level:&quot;core&quot;, scope:&quot;all&quot;) {
  let
    def ArrowGate : bool = (G &gt; 0) and (G &gt; Xi * R) ;
    def PowerGate : bool = (P &gt;= (k*T*ln2)*Bdot) ;
    def MemGate   : bool = (Irec + Imod &lt;= Imax) ;
  require ArrowGate and PowerGate and MemGate == true ;
  test under T_time_shift with cases 64 ;
  test under T_scale with cases 64 ;
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-8392-e22ee86d22d6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8721-e89de789635e" class="">1.7 Required tests (dsl_tests.py)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8a0e-e8027983a2ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Parse round-trip determinism (same AST hash every run).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-a5ca-d28a12d30107" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Typecheck rejects unknown variables, unit-incompatible ops.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-93c3-d6ddb8dd62d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Eval determinism (same inputs → same output).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-ad73-c889249dfaa7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Transform tests produce stable JSONL with deterministic ordering.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-9306-ebaf280c4082"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-a096-c15a425006fd" class="">2) TENSOR — Invariant Tensor Calculus Across Domains (Typed + Cross-Species)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-9838-d022c8cd9748" class="">2.1 Canonical files (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-bcfd-d6b112782392" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/tensors/types.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b9a7-ca2637eb7947" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/tensors/operators.py</code></li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8ffe-efb69054ae75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/tensors/metrics.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-ae96-f5f0dee33e18" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/tensors/invariants.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8f37-f1409833e6fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/tensors/tests/test_tensor_ops.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bf2c-c1c641a8bc93"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b1c6-f94b3cb8ee64" class="">2.2 Objective</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-abb5-ce6ed233433c" class="">Unify invariants into a single formal object:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8b9d-ff1060c5a6f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a <strong>state tensor</strong> that contains micro/macro layers as blocks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b5be-fadff0684a40" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transforms become tensor actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9ead-e91fadfe7db8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariants become tensor contractions that remain unchanged under allowed actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-8e27-df9682ac8755"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-aaad-dfc55db12168" class="">2.3 Typed state tensor (block structure)</h3></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-bc3d-e096cc894736" class="">Define the global state at time :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-8484-f6daa832c8f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{X}_t =
\begin{bmatrix}
\mathbf{X}^{\text{cosmo}}_t &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 \\
0 &amp; \mathbf{X}^{\text{EM}}_t &amp; 0 &amp; 0 \\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; \mathbf{X}^{\text{bio}}_t &amp; 0 \\
0 &amp; 0 &amp; 0 &amp; \mathbf{X}^{\text{soc}}_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-8b2d-efdbd17a4604" class="">Core indexing convention:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a890-de1a1c9d2208" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: discrete time index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8e6b-dae21a5be408" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: spatial cell index (grid or graph node)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-87b0-e02a78a6669f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: species index (or codebook class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8bfc-e998e262aeb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: channel index (sensor/EM/social medium)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b109-cf1cbfe8737c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: layer index (micro, meso, macro)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-9956-c5bbc6b16043" class="">So a general component:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-9e89-f815034c429d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{t,x,s,c,l}^{(d)} \in \mathbb{R}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b135-ee6c2cea5ee1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-adc1-c3da279ffead" class="">2.4 Transform group actions (what “invariance” means)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-b142-dbb125136b9e" class="">A transform  acts as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-9a80-e184f302b89d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{X}&#x27; = \mathcal{A}_T(\mathbf{X})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b50c-d5f1f77cf9a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">coordinate/basis: (where appropriate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-b9fb-c933524a9804" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">scaling: (registry supplies )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-871e-cb4de7f22b75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">species mapping: (codebook linearization)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-972d-f03c83582128" class="">Allowed transform set for invariant : .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8618-f0c3b375a045" class="">Invariant definition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a98c-c045b76f4570" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall T\in\mathcal{T}_I:\ I(\mathbf{X}) = I(\mathcal{A}_T(\mathbf{X}))}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-820f-edbba8bd8402"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-988c-cc782e4b8958" class="">2.5 Canonical invariant constructions (tensor form)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-90b9-d8a7aa1f7e70" class="">(A) Quadratic invariants (energy-like / norm-like)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b855-fd4ffc37fcbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_2(\mathbf{X}) = \langle \mathbf{X}, \mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}\rangle
= X_i M^{ij} X_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a615-ed1038b29244" class="">(B) Constraint density invariant (your “constraint-counting law”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b1a0-d44c427d7c0f" class="">Let  be a constraint residual tensor (each constraint produces a residual).<br/>Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-abb7-dfe621545948" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_t = \frac{1}{V}\sum_{x}\sum_{k} \mathbf{1}\left(|C_{t,x}^{(k)}|\le \epsilon_k\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b34a-e37413756451" class="">Arrow proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a25d-ec1be3d47b99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\Delta q_t \le 0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9ea0-ebdf8a8f837d" class="">(C) Record redundancy tensor (environment-as-code)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-983e-c8fde1e2759c" class="">Let  be redundancy stored across environment fragments.<br/>Define a stability contraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-878d-c09076062001" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_R(t) = \sum_{x,c} w_{x,c} \cdot \mathbf{1}\left(p_{t,x,c} &lt; p_{th}(r_{t,x,c})\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a424-c9c65e3d5707" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\Delta I_R(t) &gt; 0 \text{ while } U_t&gt;0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8d85-f9b62a7b2808" class="">(D) Cross-scale entailment (micro → macro)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-86e2-e0cf86d7bfc7" class="">Let  be coarse-grain operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-8b4f-ef7f65169ed1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{Y}_t = C(\mathbf{X}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-98ee-c9ecd4afeca6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\phi(\mathbf{X})=0 \Rightarrow \psi(C(\mathbf{X}))=0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8fd1-ce63b792bbc8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-9cba-eaacb69a863d" class="">2.6 Cross-species tensor lifting (codebook-aware)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-adbd-f9cc16fadaaa" class="">Species mapping as tensor operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-86af-c413f8cb36e1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X&#x27;_{s&#x27;} = \sum_s \Sigma_{s\to s&#x27;} X_s,\quad
\Sigma \text{ from species_codebook}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-badc-f8c4adcf2236" class="">Allometry normalization operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bd1c-c777d8df43aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde X_{s} = \frac{X_s}{m_s^\alpha}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a645-e1b86214885d" class="">Development normalization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-ac3d-d3230ae3f6ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat a_s = \frac{a_s}{a_{mature,s}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9e96-e24502a36750" class="">Required closure test:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-8a20-c1f3c3bd995a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{a\to c} \approx \Sigma_{b\to c}\circ \Sigma_{a\to b}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9d27-df35e508db08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-947d-c607d377edfa" class="">2.7 Tensor calculus API (operators.py)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-973c-ce377f28a1af" class="">Hard-required operators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-b458-df30e52941ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>contract(X, M, axes)</code> (general contraction)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-bd3b-d1e7b911191d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>apply_transform(X, T)</code> (group action)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-832f-e3d66fe69faf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>coarsegrain(X, C)</code> (aggregation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9c1b-e31dd9fa3604" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>lift_species(X, Sigma)</code> (codebook mapping)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-a6b0-c336d79ab75d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>normalize_allometry(X, mass, alpha)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-98c1-f77e2097dfde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>delta_time(X)</code> (discrete difference)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-8bfe-c3d0f0bb4ef7" class="">All operators must:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-97b2-cda591ca1fb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">be pure</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-9740-cd2efa35426c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-9c1c-e2028918de95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">unit/type consistent (registry-driven)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-ad50-de14bb28461e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-aa91-c2e72a518e1b" class="">3) SIMENGINE — Civilizational Control Simulation Engine (Multi-loop Coupled Dynamics)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b939-d8c2d40f5780" class="">3.1 Canonical files (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-bf1b-c6f34ddab428" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/engine.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-b9eb-e366938f8c4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/state.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b390-c2df5d5b7a6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/loops.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b307-e9d318378d20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/gates.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-8d19-dffb9c0b37bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/scenarios.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9453-ddf47c5db572" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/12_QUANTUM_LAYER/simulation/tests/test_engine.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-a68e-e4b4b19de52a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/ecosystem/sim_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a4b5-d8e5202b2e8f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a0e9-ee44db079162" class="">3.2 State (single coupled kernel)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-90fa-efce1c1040b8" class="">Define the loop-kernel state vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-ac5e-db1954c02cb5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t = (q_t,\ U_t,\ G_t,\ \Xi_t,\ R_t,\ D_t,\ P_t,\ M_t,\ K_t,\ S_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-bb08-c35975f38af3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (macro constraint satisfaction)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a447-ebb7b6e015f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: unused write-capacity (unwritten degrees)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-a42d-c54526fd3fd1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: gradient (available structured free energy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-a4a7-e124f252564a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: noise/overwrite rate (environment + adversarial)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a2dc-f57876a5d887" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record redundancy (stable copies)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b89f-cfc18c99d54a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth (stacked self-modeling depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8e5e-dc2beca8ab39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: power available</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-810d-c35392edec46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory budget (bits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a0a3-fc24ad7505ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: compressibility index (macro trace compressibility)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-b07e-e2b4b73c6527" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">: regime stage (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-ac2d-d4ff4f58562a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a34c-e1b580bf0862" class="">3.3 Dynamics (deterministic update laws)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a442-e97f2b73b718" class="">(A) Constraint unwinding / release</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9add-e5a9578f5445" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1} = \mathrm{clip}\left(q_t - \alpha_q \cdot \mathcal{U}_q(z_t),\ 0,\ 1\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b5f7-d4bb7331c721" class="">(B) Write-capacity consumption</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-ab1f-fa9e4c28672e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1} = \max(0,\ U_t - \gamma_U \cdot \Delta R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-95be-d08f971ccd45" class="">(C) Gradient evolution</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-bf93-dca25c3d2a15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1} = \mathrm{clip}\left(G_t + \alpha_G \cdot \mathcal{S}(q_t) - \beta_G \cdot \mathcal{D}(R_t,\Xi_t),\ 0,\ G_{\max}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8a67-ea2a282eadc8" class="">(D) Noise evolution</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b449-cfe352f43ade" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1} = \mathrm{clip}\left(\Xi_t + \alpha_\Xi \cdot \mathcal{A}(S_t) - \beta_\Xi \cdot \mathcal{H}(P_t),\ 0,\ \Xi_{\max}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-99fd-fd31ed3e2eb3" class="">(E) Record redundancy update (with phase transition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-a46a-e506cc0d1c45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1} = R_t + \beta_R G_t - \kappa_R \Xi_t R_t - \lambda_R \cdot \mathbf{1}\left(\Xi_t \ge \Xi_{th}(r_t)\right)\cdot R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-8fc6-c46a420a46ad" class="">(F) Recursion depth update (repair vs delay stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-bc9f-cb2db1e734a8" class="">Define per-depth stability gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a8dd-db428c150b80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stable}_D \equiv \bigwedge_{d\le D}\left[\alpha_d - \rho_d \cdot \phi(\tau_d) &lt; 1\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8610-c0f99a6e3437" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1} =
\begin{cases}
D_t + 1 &amp; \text{if } \mathrm{Stable}_D \land \mathrm{BudgetOK} \land \Delta R_t&gt;0\\
D_t     &amp; \text{if } \mathrm{BudgetOK}\\
\max(0, D_t - 1) &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bdca-f9ac01b1b6d3" class="">(G) Budget gates (hard)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-8e69-e02f695e73d3" class="">Landauer:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-8fe7-fd94b7febb33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b7ef-f1ad9a0e797c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{records}}(R_t)+I_{\text{models}}(D_t)\le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-985c-e19c68805905" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_t \le I_h(H_t)\ \propto\ \frac{1}{H_t^2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-a5d2-e6269f8e28f3" class="">(H) Compressibility of history (macro-trace)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-ad8a-d71cd41a4019" class="">Let  be compressibility index proxy (lower = more compressible):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-a38f-eed59ca3d6fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K_{t+1} = K_t - \eta_K \cdot q_t + \zeta_K \cdot \Xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-b40a-c44a403d0143"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-9cf9-e6ee4d07b6cc" class="">3.4 Regime scheduler (Birth→Expansion→Dominance→Decay)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a2db-dffab319ea27" class="">Define stage as deterministic function of :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-a308-fb0c2e1ab03b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Birth: high , high , low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9386-e24dc58b00f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Expansion: rising and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8b7f-dd65245a9376" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dominance: high, falling,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-8c1c-de42dcbe3c3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decay: high or falling and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a7bb-fed03032b3db" class="">One deterministic classifier:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-8212-f1360f11ff2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=\arg\max_{s\in\{B,E,Dc,De\}} \mathrm{score}_s(z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-88da-cd9ccc15d47e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-93fa-e9ebc03c79cc" class="">3.5 Multi-loop coupling (civilization as coupled subsystems)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a4d7-dd527771bff3" class="">Civilization is not one loop; it is a coupled graph of loop-instances:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b29f-e618ea7cf43b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">energy loop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8e7e-d0796ef46c9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">record loop (archives, media, institutions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-9444-ea02e9ffdbae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cognition loop (collective modeling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-88e4-d8d623a3e1c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM loop (communications + interference)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-91e1-cf7cdce47ee0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biological loop (population health/repair capacity)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a77e-df3dc617e743" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adversarial loop (deception/overwrite)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-8e43-f1c384edfa3f" class="">Represent as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-afab-efd5388355ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t^{(i)}\quad,\quad z_{t+1}^{(i)} = F_i(z_t^{(i)},\ \sum_j W_{ij} z_t^{(j)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-92ff-c49c30a4a4ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8d54-d6c77627bf87" class="">3.6 Outputs (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-b5fc-f9c4527b3ca5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_trace.jsonl</code> (one line per step: full )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-8905-fb0b792c737c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_gates.jsonl</code> (pass/fail per gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b8b5-c32f74384375" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_regimes.jsonl</code> (stage per step)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a879-c94b870d8f31" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_invariants.jsonl</code> (invariant evaluation per step)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a76f-c2a2478ccbf7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_report.json</code> (summary + termination classification)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a88d-fff237586c02" class="">Termination classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-9d59-d8029c414405" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong>: all required gates pass for scenario set + invariants stable under transforms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8e06-f29fa2fd1a4d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong>: explicit missing observables / codebook c
onflicts / untyped support</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-baa9-e6cff4b8945e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong>: invariant contradictions or transform failures in required scope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-84d9-fdbb6c17dbef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-869f-dcd0147c4cc0" class="">What this “All 3” adds beyond prior layers</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8224-e09dfbbddcc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DSL makes invariants executable and testable (no informal math).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-be46-d6dd906f770d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tensor layer makes invariants coordinate/species/scale aware and composable.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-be46-d167144b45fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SimEngine makes the loop kernel dynamic, coupled, regime-aware, and gate-audited.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bde5-cf7dd3ad2b2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-a18e-fd151335364e" class="">Next (no questions; pick one word when ready)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-bd41-daf76970250d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>IMPLEMENT</strong> → file-level implementation plan + exact module/function/test list (AMOS-ready)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-bdce-f4893e4aa912" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CATALOG</strong> → exhaustive invariant template library (200+ templates) in DSL form</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-9991-c1ece554490f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SCENARIOS</strong> → full scenario suite (birth/expansion/dominance/decay, adversarial, horizon-cap, EM interference) with deterministic configs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8734-d220b1494438" class="">1) REGISTRY — Single Variable Registry (SSOT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-989b-f7a7b44d58e6" class="">1.1 Purpose</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a47f-cb529e23ccda" class="">One canonical registry that defines <strong>every</strong> variable AMOS is allowed to reason over, across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-9921-d2537328b3f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">physics, cosmology, planet, EM, biology, cognition, social/civilization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a26f-fab711780f11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">micro ↔ macro</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a46c-c2f4a080f244" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cross-species mappings (via CODEBOOK)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-baf9-e0ee0a95802e" class="">Registry is the only place that establishes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a0f5-c957d6da1414" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>units</strong>, <strong>type</strong>, <strong>observability</strong>, <strong>allowed transforms</strong>, <strong>causal dependencies</strong>, <strong>scope</strong>, <strong>privacy/ownership flags</strong>, <strong>evidence requirements</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-a7aa-d6f7d919e784"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-9e86-ea117047b6f6" class="">1.2 Canonical location (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-bb08-df3e22dac4c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/variable_registry/registry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-87c5-e75e41698815" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/variable_registry/schema.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a323-c8efc3d687cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/variable_registry/units.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-8da4-c123a952b768" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/variable_registry/domains.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-bd4b-fabc586f2f6c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-aee3-e4e5e154b0eb" class="">1.3 Minimal schema (hard-required fields)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-ab65-d58a5f5bf390" class="">A variable  is a tuple:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-8e44-dadf4d778119" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
v_i=\langle \text{id},\ \text{name},\ \text{domain},\ \text{layer},\ \text{dtype},\ \text{units},\ \text{space},\ \text{time},\ \text{species},\ \text{obs},\ \mathcal{T},\ \text{deps},\ \text{range},\ \text{evidence}\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-88eb-d3e7833100cc" class=""><strong>registry.json (one record per variable)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/components/prism-json.min.js" integrity="sha512-QXFMVAusM85vUYDaNgcYeU3rzSlc+bTV4JvkfJhjxSHlQEo+ig53BtnGkvFTiNJh8D+wv6uWAQ2vJaVmxe8d3w==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a4e2-fc938c1d1062" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;id&quot;: &quot;v.em.snr&quot;,
  &quot;name&quot;: &quot;signal_to_noise_ratio&quot;,
  &quot;domain&quot;: &quot;electromagnetic&quot;,
  &quot;layer&quot;: &quot;environment&quot;,
  &quot;dtype&quot;: &quot;float64&quot;,
  &quot;units&quot;: &quot;dimensionless&quot;,
  &quot;space&quot;: {&quot;frame&quot;: &quot;observer&quot;, &quot;support&quot;: &quot;local&quot;},
  &quot;time&quot;: {&quot;index&quot;: &quot;t&quot;, &quot;step&quot;: &quot;discrete&quot;},
  &quot;species&quot;: {&quot;scope&quot;: &quot;all&quot;, &quot;codebook_key&quot;: &quot;em_comm&quot;},
  &quot;observability&quot;: {
    &quot;class&quot;: &quot;measured&quot;,
    &quot;channels&quot;: [&quot;instrument&quot;],
    &quot;latency_steps&quot;: 0,
    &quot;noise_model&quot;: &quot;gaussian&quot;
  },
  &quot;allowed_transforms&quot;: [&quot;T_coord&quot;, &quot;T_time_shift&quot;, &quot;T_channel&quot;, &quot;T_representation&quot;],
  &quot;dependencies&quot;: [&quot;v.em.bandwidth&quot;, &quot;v.em.signal_power&quot;, &quot;v.em.noise_power&quot;],
  &quot;range&quot;: {&quot;min&quot;: 0.0, &quot;max&quot;: null},
  &quot;evidence&quot;: {
    &quot;support_types&quot;: [&quot;Empirical&quot;, &quot;Model-bounded&quot;],
    &quot;required_artifacts&quot;: [&quot;measurement_trace&quot;, &quot;calibration_meta&quot;],
    &quot;ownership&quot;: {&quot;has_owner&quot;: true, &quot;owner_class&quot;: &quot;system&quot;}
  }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-bb16-dfd9144467ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-9974-feb43a14c994" class="">1.4 Registry invariants (must always hold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-91c1-e5e8c4fda895" class=""><strong>R1 — Unit consistency</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b1d6-f5415a5b2f40" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall v:\ \mathrm{units}(v)\ \text{valid} \Rightarrow \mathrm{dim}(v)\ \text{well-defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-941f-c282362d1134" class=""><strong>R2 — Transform permission</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-ae18-f756193de2fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T\in \mathcal{T}(v)\ \Leftrightarrow\ \text{registry explicitly lists }T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-ac12-daa59c71a77d" class=""><strong>R3 — Observability typing</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-ba5b-de86d3844e72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{obs.class}\in\{\text{measured, inferred, latent, unobservable}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a018-d77edbfb3630" class=""><strong>R4 — Ownership compatibility</strong><br/>If <code>ownership.has_owner=true</code>, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9884-d7e4a69c67de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{no export of raw artifacts without policy gate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-b729-f731c05902cc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a1e1-c808c8022460" class="">1.5 Domain blocks (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-bb13-c4f1985fa68d" class="">Registry must define at least these blocks (each with 50–500 variables as needed):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-a1bb-d1d2d0ddd09e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cosmic/Gravity</strong>: curvature proxies, expansion proxies, horizon proxies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-bb65-d89862818b19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Planet/Earth</strong>: rotation, geomagnetism proxies, climate gradients, biosphere cycles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-9c28-feb883e3af49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EM</strong>: bandwidth, SNR, channel capacity, interference, coding distance proxies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bbd2-dbe4099bfcbd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Biology</strong>: metabolic power, repair capacity, error rates, allometry factors, developmental stage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-b0b6-da32e5a5e28b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cognition</strong>: working memory, attention bandwidth, model error, recursion depth, delay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-a17b-c191a01af921" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Social/Civilization</strong>: institutions, write-capacity layers (archives/media), n
oise/propaganda rate, resource gradients</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a32c-c970f5ffd172"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-8142-dfada63e775b" class="">2) TRANSFORMS — Full Transform Library + Test Harness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-8f01-dc7906b07b54" class="">2.1 Purpose</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a531-d1d72a15e7c0" class="">A transform  is an allowed change of representation/frame/scale that <strong>must not break</strong> an invariant unless the invariant explicitly declares it is not required to survive it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-b66e-cf0329871849"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-bb64-e4b10b21405b" class="">2.2 Canonical location (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a52d-c85761a9a382" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/DSL/transforms.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9067-d866883faf87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/validators/transform_tests.py</code> (or .py module)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b7b9-df598bb991a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-b1b1-dbb8a49bb8fe" class="">2.3 Transform definition schema</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-aad0-df99ef204768" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;id&quot;: &quot;T_time_shift&quot;,
  &quot;class&quot;: &quot;time&quot;,
  &quot;description&quot;: &quot;shift time index by Δ&quot;,
  &quot;parameters&quot;: [&quot;delta_t&quot;],
  &quot;applies_to&quot;: [&quot;time_indexed_variables&quot;],
  &quot;constraints&quot;: {&quot;delta_t_min&quot;: -1000000, &quot;delta_t_max&quot;: 1000000},
  &quot;inverse_exists&quot;: true,
  &quot;test_generators&quot;: [&quot;gen_time_series&quot;, &quot;gen_events&quot;],
  &quot;expected_invariants&quot;: [&quot;time_translation_invariants&quot;]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-812e-ce8e6bb3e2b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-aa40-e414531f560a" class="">2.4 Required transform set (kernel-complete)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a95f-e0ad63945c29" class="">A) Geometry / physics</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b5d4-c4d9b86179c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: coordinate/basis change</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-945f-efb7a7b9d805" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: (declared per-unit dimension)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-b106-c0a60d59237e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-86c5-d613d65e9404" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9afc-e4bd4133bc6f" class="">B) Information / EM</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-af7f-dc05f1a62e5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: channel model change preserving payload</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-aa34-fa58c099b914" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: representation change</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a059-c1f93032f39f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: codebook change (same message class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8606-c2798affb182" class="">C) Biology / species</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-9cab-ef39c28a458b" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: homolog mapping via CODEBOOK</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-926d-f78932553b71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a980-f0c3892ba295" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stage-index normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-952e-ebf7e9df8ff7" class="">D) Cognition / computation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-85bf-ef1e8f728115" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: embedding/representation change</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-8646-e11f5b8ae114" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: reparameterization of control law (same behavior class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-8e70-dac0315ad14f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: encoding change preserving retrieval function</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-9c30-fccf5db9e81b" class="">E) Social / civilization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bad2-e2b0355c38ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: symbol relabeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-976e-e2c668ac2fa5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: currency/numeraire change</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-a7fb-c2b5e92dac5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: role/function reparameterization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-8d54-c3be97fccb85"/></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b701-ff0ff14c2162" class="">2.5 Transform test harness (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-adf6-eb6e83fb4c7c" class="">For each invariant  with predicate  and transform set :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-b522-da8d63777164" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall T\in\mathcal{T}_I,\ \forall x\in \mathcal{X}_I:\ \phi_I(x)=0\ \Rightarrow\ \phi_I(Tx)=0
