}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-b252-d6e20dc1fc45" class="">Practical test generation uses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-ad4c-f9cf3fda0687" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic seeds from hashes (no randomness):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-b8ca-c7f2590eec07" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{seed}(I,T)=\mathrm{sha256}(I\|T)[:8]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b2ba-f6d167c2e901" class="">Test case object:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-9947-c7bad610ca4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;invariant_id&quot;: &quot;I.records.monotone&quot;,
  &quot;transform_id&quot;: &quot;T_time_shift&quot;,
  &quot;case_id&quot;: &quot;sha256(...)&quot;,
  &quot;inputs_hash&quot;: &quot;sha256(x)&quot;,
  &quot;result&quot;: &quot;pass|fail&quot;,
  &quot;delta&quot;: {&quot;phi_before&quot;: 0.0, &quot;phi_after&quot;: 0.0}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-9cd6-cba6a90a9239"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9bb7-d21021ff903a" class="">3) CODEBOOK — Cross-Species / Cross-System Mapping</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8ea5-d3c752340acc" class="">3.1 Purpose</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-87a1-f41ebb349266" class="">Formalize “same functional variable” across species/systems so invariants can be tested across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-894f-fdbd32e9dffd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">different bodies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-ad3c-fed9f7175503" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">different nervous systems</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-bbe0-e68cd68f95e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">different sensor/actuator morphologies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bfc6-d4a5da82398b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">different lifecycles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b770-f1868520acce"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-a425-e677882b8916" class="">3.2 Canonical location (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-a949-e388b70fe26d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/canonical_definitions/species_codebook.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-a476-c165dbd354f6" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/canonical_definitions/allometry.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-a20b-ed809da50f0c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/canonical_definitions/homology_graph.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a64a-c15cffd60850"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bdac-d4f09b420b54" class="">3.3 Mapping formalism</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-8f70-f424f6f47efe" class="">Define a homology mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-94b2-e721e6e04542" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma:\ (v,\ s_a)\ \mapsto\ (v&#x27;,\ s_b,\ f_{\Sigma})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-bf64-f3a838c6cffb" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-b6e4-ea38f0ec9923" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma(\text{heart\_rate},\ \text{human})\mapsto(\text{pulse\_rate},\ \text{dog},\ f(m,\text{stage}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-91dc-d03e1a763823"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-bcb0-f6cdb04219a9" class="">3.4 Allometry normalization (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-9e96-e7205dd44e0b" class="">For many bio variables, compare via:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-b5e8-eea7352e8427" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\tilde v = \frac{v}{m^\alpha}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-94b6-c65d2c5ee00c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-92d6-ca9678466911" class="">3.5 Development-stage normalization (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-b577-e00bccdf1f37" class="">Define stage  or continuous age .<br/>A normalized stage index:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b274-f5892fbcebf5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\hat a = \frac{a}{a_{\text{mature}}}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-be98-e54c8dde5d3f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-85a4-c50c8de8662d" class="">3.6 Homology graph (closure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-be07-e5ed5c476048" class="">Variables form a graph:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b2a3-eac2decd0648" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">nodes: variable ids scoped by species</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-a6cc-dcfdbea3a38e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edges: mapping functions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-84e1-c6242c5996bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">required property: compositional closure when possible</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-a866-c2c1bb62e0f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\Sigma_{a\to c} \approx \Sigma_{b\to c}\circ \Sigma_{a\to b}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-8283-d3bda1fbff41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-921d-e90cde81bbf5" class="">3.7 Ownership / access layer (for “information has owners”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bb70-c455269ef78d" class="">Every mapping carries an access tag:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bb62-f8cb9e2d5de7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;codebook_key&quot;: &quot;em_comm&quot;,
  &quot;owner_class&quot;: &quot;system|person|institution|unknown&quot;,
  &quot;access&quot;: &quot;public|restricted|private&quot;,
  &quot;allowed_uses&quot;: [&quot;aggregate_only&quot;, &quot;no_export_raw&quot;]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-867b-c9dba5b63a10"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9f90-d74b176d16ff" class="">4) SEARCH — Deterministic Invariant Discovery Algorithm (Operator Closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8b65-d8a0da9d6a53" class="">4.1 Purpose</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-bd92-dbe1c13fc718" class="">Given data + definitions, produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-bdac-f88bd43e00d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">candidate invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8e01-dd165ce5afa0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">proofs/tests per invariant</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-b6e5-f844fb208f2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transform survival results</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-be2b-efe327d1d3be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">termination classification (Valid/Bounded/Invalid)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-9813-d373d32821c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">next fixes (if Bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-9bb4-ee8ed5603bd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-b1a0-f219c326dc2d" class="">4.2 Canonical location (SSOT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9d92-c52ae467f20b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/08_WORLD_MODEL/models/runner.py</code> (execution)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8830-e1732599d470" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/15_LAW_ENGINE/structural_integrity/invariant_search.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-88a4-d3210322a162" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>/Users/trangphan/AMOS/01_BRAIN/kernel/termination.py</code> (classification)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9ebf-d2802aebb5b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b007-f499bdfb9369" class="">4.3 Inputs (what search consumes)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-9268-f424db7208e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">variable registry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9192-c189300f440e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transform library</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-97f6-e12325def3ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">codebook (species mappings)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-8a56-d250426fe357" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ingestion artifacts (signals, text definitions, event logs)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-9378-c4275144f35d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">existing invariants (seed set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9004-fcd139e164ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-b1ef-f180b499200c" class="">4.4 Candidate generator (operators that generate invariants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-8de0-e5909773d9f5" class="">Define operator s
et:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-8178-fb811437ccd7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{O}=\{\mathcal{S},\mathcal{N},\mathcal{C},\mathcal{E},\mathcal{G},\mathcal{K}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9f51-f2f2c7c4c8f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Symmetry/orbit invariance: constant on transform orbits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-84fa-e8f471e40342" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Noether-like: symmetry ⇒ conserved quantity (where applicable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-a61a-d70213e068a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coarse-grain entailment: macro invariant entailed by micro</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-b4a6-eadd21161fd7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Elimination: eliminate latents to produce observable constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a498-ed8ec5df8f3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Gate synthesis: derive inequalities from budget gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-b7b4-ef6d70cb1311" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Kernel coupling: derive cross-layer invariants (bio↔EM↔social)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9161-e2eaf3a99408" class="">Candidate form templates (must be supported by DSL):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-85b4-e8e5ebffcc51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">equality:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-9694-d229c704a937" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">inequality:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-b0c1-d8b82b3fe2b9" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">monotone:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-8ad0-de578199f488" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">threshold/phase:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9be9-f8dfc95203e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-a665-eb975c79099a" class="">4.5 Scoring (deterministic, no randomness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-9a10-cf91f4b5c2e5" class="">For candidate :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b875-f0d1141484b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathrm{Score}(I)=w_1\cdot \mathrm{Coverage}
+w_2\cdot \mathrm{TransformSurvival}
+w_3\cdot \mathrm{Simplicity}
+w_4\cdot \mathrm{SupportStrength}
-w_5\cdot \mathrm{AssumptionLoad}
-w_6\cdot \mathrm{Unobservables}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9172-f358a8d536a3" class="">Definitions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-92bc-ec63f5e16f1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coverage: number of domains/layers/species where it applies (from registry + codebook)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b9b9-d4bf19d8ee76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TransformSurvival: fraction of passed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-ad99-c52f55fc5bf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Simplicity: description length proxy (AST node count)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-80a6-c2ae70860981" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SupportStrength: typed support (Empirical &gt; Inferential &gt; Model-bounded &gt; Primitive &gt; Limit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-bf08-da9202fb31ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AssumptionLoad: number of explicit assumptions required</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-87b6-fd73e2c913dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unobservables: count of latent-only variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-ab88-c8d0d78ab74e" class="">Tie-break rule: lexicographic on .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-a013-ca199bdfa271"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-9aeb-c6df989595f6" class="">4.6 Proof obligations / gates (must pass)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9a6f-c398c407b26f" class=""><strong>G1 — Typing gate</strong></p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9b46-d46ca8cea505" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau(I)\ \text{assigned and non-load-bearing Limit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-b640-d5d0ca5c2e94" class=""><strong>G2 — Transform gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-ba33-fcb344d00979" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall T\in\mathcal{T}_I:\ \mathrm{Test}(I,T)=\text{pass}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a5bd-da8416255a37" class=""><strong>G3 — Cross-grain gate (micro→macro)</strong><br/>If macro invariant depends on coarse graining :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-be78-fd6c457aa5dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\phi_{\text{macro}}(C(x))=0\ \text{must be implied by}\ \phi_{\text{micro}}(x)=0
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8a6a-ffce0f0d8c6b" class=""><strong>G4 — Budget gates (Landauer + memory + write-capacity)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-b06b-c37047e1eccc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D),\quad
I_{\text{records}}+I_{\text{models}}\le I_{\max},\quad
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma\Delta R,\ U\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9f89-c4c75fa1a71f" class=""><strong>G5 — Ownership gate</strong><br/>If owner tag is restricted, invariant may be retained but raw evidence cannot be exported.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-909f-c80f47c0f329"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-8739-cdb08e9c82d6" class="">4.7 Closure / fixed-point condition (“invariants of invariance”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a3e3-dbea9cd7d04f" class="">A stable invariant set  satisfies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-96d2-f6c43428e27d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal{I}^\* = \mathrm{Close}_{\mathcal{O}}(\mathcal{I}^\*)\ \cap\ \mathrm{PassGates}(\mathcal{I}^\*)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9337-e5cbf735d578" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no new invariant improves score beyond epsilon, and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-8b0c-c8cfbc0327b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">all invariants are gate-consistent.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8efa-c76ca8b68548"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-9a3e-dfec82bffd39" class="">4.8 Outputs (required artifacts)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8d9e-ea37fb84b2ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>invariants.jsonl</code> (each invariant with scope, transforms, tests, typing, supports)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8525-c6e9e10fb0f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>transform_tests.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-a01b-c26014a05ed7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>closure_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-9f80-db5aa4dd11c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-a108-f2be73515eaa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>issues.jsonl</code> (BLOCKER/MAJOR/MINOR with exact failed gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8107-f237a45e360b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-97df-c67729390ed5" class="">Immediate “what we missed” that these four close</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol t
ype="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9a58-c741996ac964" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>SSOT variable semantics</strong> (without registry, everything floats)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-99ec-e09044535a7a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Explicit transform survival</strong> (without transforms, “invariant” is undefined)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8ce0-e1ca144a086d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Cross-species comparability</strong> (without codebook, claims don’t generalize)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8487-c94cc236fce3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Deterministic discovery + closure</strong> (without search, you only hand-author invariants)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b0d7-cfcd747e9409"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-b6a2-f455c9fee50f" class="">Next (if you want the next layer after All 4)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a9ec-f8c132dbab80" class="">One word, and I will output it fully:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a83d-c693bb254d81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DSL</strong> (full invariant DSL grammar: equations, inequalities, monotones, thresholds, gates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-b0f8-c609f7ab9ef5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TENSOR</strong> (tensor calculus layer to unify multi-domain invariants as typed tensors)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9dcd-e210cdf00273" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SIMENGINE</strong> (civilizational c
ontrol simulation engine wiring end-to-end)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9eb3-d9612675d581" class="">KERNELSPEC — InvariantKernelSpec v1 (SSOT, implementable, exhaustive)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a1d4-fa85695a1c86" class="">0) Purpose</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-9df4-c6b81e8e376d" class="">Build a single kernel that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-9cff-d6ccec2e3b81" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>extracts candidate invariants</strong> from multi-domain data (physics, biology, cognition, EM, society, history),</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-93dc-ebb6ccf86df2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>types</strong> every claim and support,</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-98db-c24dd80ce36c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>tests</strong> invariance under declared transforms,</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-8c5f-f4d188456358" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>closes</strong> via an operator algebra (invariants generate invariants),</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a581-edde6ac89822" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>terminates</strong> into <strong>Valid / Bounded / Invalid</strong> with explicit reasons.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-bc97-cb978231ab3a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b708-c850a66f5832" class="">1) Core objects</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-bb88-d67f06da136e" class="">1.1 State bundle (cross-time, c
ross-space)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b2e3-ca28e05a2e93" class="">Let the world be a base manifold  (space-time + index axes), and a fiber  containing all domain states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-8b33-e06aa424663f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi:\ \mathcal{B}=\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{F}\ \to\ \mathcal{M}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-bc9e-d26122ce841f" class="">Discrete-time implementation uses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-9abe-e6f786b1ccdd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t,u_t,\xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-a58d-cd70390bc2f3" class="">1.2 Domain partitions (typed state vector)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a757-da84cddf1c2d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=\big(x_t^{\text{phys}},x_t^{\text{bio}},x_t^{\text{cog}},x_t^{\text{em}},x_t^{\text{soc}},x_t^{\text{planet}},x_t^{\text{cosmic}}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-8ec9-c14931e71747" class="">Each block has a schema in the variable registry.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-8f79-f34947a257cf" class="">1.3 Invariant object</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-ad6a-ef12a4b75f0e" class="">An invariant is a predicate + scope + transform-set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9454-dd975726f8a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = \langle \phi_I,\ \mathcal{D}_I,\ \mathcal{T}_I,\ \tau(I),\ \mathrm{support}(I)\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8264-da58c1b06c63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: domain + time/space window + species set + environment layers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-a88a-d9a7d3acd977" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: transform library subset it must survive</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-960e-ce7b9fd76653" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: claim type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-b68c-e62165d78769" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">support: proofs/data/derivations (typed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-8568-d74a1011400d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-8787-e3fc17a3c89d" class="">2) Law-of-Law gates (meta invariants)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-9cea-e0d5c7a634f8" class="">2.1 Non-contradiction under declared transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-bee0-c07679adb812" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall T\in\mathcal{T}_I,\ \phi_I(x)=0 \Rightarrow \phi_I(Tx)=0
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8ef0-f6186647125e" class="">2.2 Total claim typing (UCIA-compatible)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-907a-d6790d6d0575" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\tau(c)\in\{\text{Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Model-bounded, Primitive, Limit}\}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b27f-d756158eb2fd" class="">2.3 No load-bearing “Limit”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8df3-c5058ca3cbb3" class="">If  is required to conclude a hard result:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-864c-f76a12393099" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{c\notin \text{Limit}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-861a-d74b765eef9f" class="">2.4 Refinement transport (ontology updates must preserve invariants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-823d-f24c888b9449" class="">If ontology refines via mapping :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b0a0-c71a261c720a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall I\in\mathcal{I}_t,\ \exists I&#x27;\in\mathcal{I}_{t+1}:\ I&#x27; \circ \rho_t = I}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-8cce-e850e746368f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-88bd-c5c2b75f2c71" class="">3) Transform library (the missing closure piece)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-abc4-ed80d5b75189" class="">3.1 Physical transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b917-cf7bf2085fd3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: coordinate changes / basis changes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-84db-d45a453cd60e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-8d60-c8587434a12b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: (declared per variable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a1d2-f25908d1c436" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: coarse-graining maps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-abb8-c1fe38cee83a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: measurement frame transforms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-984e-c9f60b26895c" class="">3.2 Biological transforms (cross-species / cross-body)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-9747-fbd66fe008e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: mapping between homologous variables (heart-rate ↔ metabolic rate, etc.) via a codebook</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-84ba-de7707eb6307" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: scaling by mass/size:</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a938-c0bd7a19c82c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: age-stage index transform</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a1ae-c5bfc62ea7a6" class="">3.3 Cognitive transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-b680-c252360683a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy reparameterization (same behavior class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a17c-f4ef8f058483" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: embedding change that preserves decision equivalence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-af5b-dfc4632d3c8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory encoding changes preserving recall functional</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-8564-edf6c5c74bde" class="">3.4 EM / information transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-806d-dac7723c2639" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: channel coding transform preserving payload invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-bf1e-cf97eed86a7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: capacity scaling with SNR constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b14f-e0dcec0ffd5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: representation change (same information)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a3aa-e8d0ad1ff2c1" class="">3.5 Social / civilizational transforms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9b4f-fd85c7162fe9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: symbol relabeling (
ontology)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-87f0-edc0cba82067" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: role reparameterization preserving function</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9465-f6ee8b3b27fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: numeraire transform (value invariants)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-96e4-d35a3476f7d8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-ae6e-d603c28bfaea" class="">4) Environment stack (nested write-capacity + noise)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-827e-d0840af14a2b" class="">Define environment layers :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b1bd-ec9b6941b276" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t = (\Xi_t^{(0)},...,\Xi_t^{(5)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-931d-e7a8fe4ac91d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t = (U_t^{(0)},...,U_t^{(5)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b5b7-fa31ccde49e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t = (R_t^{(0)},...,R_t^{(5)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-a5dd-c5d6d6feadf6" class="">Capacity consumption:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8054-c2b165be59ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
U_{t+1}^{(k)} = U_t^{(k)} - \gamma^{(k)}\Delta R_t^{(k)} \quad;\quad U_t^{(k)}\ge 0
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a25f-e2113d7d829b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-a994-c4f58d948318" class="">5) The Loop Kernel state (Grand Unified Loop vector)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-9afb-fa37df7f1c57" class="">Define the kernel state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-878a-c1b28e9edab8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal{L}_t=\big(q_t,\ W_t,\ G_t,\ U_t,\ \Xi_t,\ R_t,\ D_t,\ P_t,\ M_t,\ \tau_t\big)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9ef5-e052b273eac3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (initial boundary constraints, generalized)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a96b-d36ddd61625e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: “free gravitational DOF” proxy (Weyl-like freedom proxy in domain)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-9f93-c1847676d8e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: gradient availability vector (free energy / value gradients / information gradients)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-b986-d9fdbbe3920b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: write-capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b2b5-c0a1edaa5669" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: noise/overwrite pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-af27-f5a7bd9e9393" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stable record redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-bd01-c7eab8ebcd13" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8f15-f890b5fb3844" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available power budget (per domain)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-b3e0-f94de8990907" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: memory budget (bounded by region/horizon constraints)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b6df-dfc6f6a845e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: regime stage (
Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b8ae-c3f7e36b646e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a3f2-e4bd8566e9cc" class="">6) Update equations (deterministic, gated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-b438-dcaf124f2c4f" class="">6.1 Constraint unwinding law (replaces “entropy” scalar)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a887-d8a36e254013" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
q_{t+1}=q_t-\delta_q\cdot \Psi(\mathcal{L}_t)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9518-ca78007bc87d" class="">6.2 Weyl-like freedom growth proxy (domain-agnostic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-93a2-ec69ebec4ae0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
W_{t+1}=W_t+\delta_W\cdot \Psi(\mathcal{L}_t)-\chi_W\cdot \mathrm{repair}_t
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8fab-c0890fca1d01" class="">6.3 Gradient availability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-b79e-ebf8060560df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
G_{t+1}=G_t + \nabla_{\text{in}} - \nabla_{\text{diss}} - \nabla_{\text{exhaust}}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-aaf6-e120c45c12c6" class="">6.4 Record growth with EM/social coupling matrix</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-bf86-dc3b9139d80c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
R_{t+1} = R_t + \beta\odot G_t - (\kappa\odot \Xi_t)\odot R_t + K R_t
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-94e8-c11e158ea56f" class="">6.5 Code-threshold (“record phase transition”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b82e-d02674941ad6" class="">Let  be effective corruption rate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-99e6-d894dfdee2fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
p(\Xi_t^{(k)})&lt;p_{\text{th}}(r_t^{(k)})\ \Rightarrow\ \text{records stable in layer }k
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-afd6-d0327f563c70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}^{(k)} \leftarrow (1-\lambda^{(k)})R_{t}^{(k)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-a58a-c77013ea481b" class="">6.6 Recursion depth dynamics (error-correction budget)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-b5ca-f5232da4a49a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\varepsilon_{t+1}^{(d)}=\alpha_d \varepsilon_t^{(d)}+\eta_d(t)-r_d(t-\tau_d)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-ab76-cb70cfcf5fa4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall d\le D_t:\ \sup_t \varepsilon_t^{(d)}\le \epsilon_d
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-b325-f37d5958cf90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
D_{t+1}=D_t + \mathbf{1}[\text{all depth gates pass}] - \mathbf{1}[\text{any depth gate fails}]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b3df-cbf3245fee93" class="">6.7 Landauer compute budget gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-97f4-c7a420450bc1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
P_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-8fad-cd68c9a3e831" class="">6.8 Memory bound gate (region / horizon / system limit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-a45f-f14b2874238b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
I_{\text{records}}(R_t)+I_{\text{models}}(D_t)\le I_{\max}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-9e43-c06478e662e3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8ce8-f4c083a0f85d" class="">7) Regime classifier (Birth → Expansion → Dominance → Decay)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-96cc-f109bad5a631" class="">Define a regime score vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b90d-c36346107d49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_t=\big(q_t,\ U_t,\ G_t,\ \Xi_t,\ R_t,\ D_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-b171-ebb349fe6d29" class="">Hard rules:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-a224-c7dc7f7304d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Birth</strong>: high, high,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-97c3-d96c33801b1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Expansion</strong>: and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-ae89-d880ef2a4651" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dominance</strong>: and saturating</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a0a6-df277790e1ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Decay</strong>: in enough layers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-af4e-e688eb76574f" class="">One explicit gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8a40-f6b30b155501" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\text{Decay if } \sum_k \mathbf{1}[\beta^{(k)}G^{(k)}\le \kappa^{(k)}\Xi^{(k)}R^{(k)}] \ge \theta_{\text{decay}}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-999b-f5cd1843b1be"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b23b-cf8ec80e9ccf" class="">8) Self / non-self boundary (immune-grade definition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-8385-cbea4cb255d6" class="">For an agent , variable  is “self” iff controllable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8a25-cb368a790971" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
v\in \text{Self}(A)\iff \exists \pi:\ \mathbb{E}\|v_{t+\Delta}-v^\star\|\le \epsilon
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-96dc-cb5060e9120b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-931a-d6d17266fb0e" class="">9) Consciousness / awareness functional closure (structural, testable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a7f2-cb0c46074b07" class="">Define workspace , memory , attention , policy , report .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-9b14-c430eb1b25fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\begin{aligned}
y_t &amp;= h(x_t,z_t)+\nu_t \\
W_{t+1} &amp;= f(W_t,y_t,M_t,A_t) \\
r_t &amp;= g(W_t) \\
u_t &amp;= \pi(W_t,M_t) \\
M_{t+1} &amp;= \mathcal{L}(M_t,W_t,y_t)
\end{aligned}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-aaac-f6f070a96f05"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8f30-d65584bb6556" class="">10) Operator algebra (invariants generate invariants)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a285-d228ceb71ba5" class="">10.1 Base operators</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-b7ce-e6fc7432ae20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Elimination</strong> : eliminate latents</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-8358-d51357e86848" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Symmetry</strong> : invariants constant on orbits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-88e0-cf05cbd090dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Noether</strong> : symmetry → conserved current</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-9332-dd0e9acb0c9a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Coarse-grain entailment</strong> : macro invariant must follow from micro</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b4eb-e3a6cba6aeef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\Omega = \text{Search}\circ(\mathcal{E},\mathcal{S},\mathcal{N},\mathcal{C})\circ\text{Gates}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-aba4-f1087ffa5817" class="">10.2 Closure rule</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-9788-de1bae010f7a" class="">A newly generated invariant  is admissible iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b846-d560d9894031" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathrm{LawOfLaw}(I&#x27;)\land \mathrm{Type}(I&#x27;)\land \mathrm{TransformTest}(I&#x27;)\land \mathrm{Support}(I&#x27;)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-9ffd-c1e313512eca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b32a-f7e9d822bdde" class="">11) Evidence + support typing (needed for “max accuracy”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-bf35-da3b00c7e8b9" class="">Support objects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-8e3e-ff0e3906d6d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data trace</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-a4c3-fc6f4b515ce4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Derivation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8c05-c664104318dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Definition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b523-fb95eae38dc6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Simulation result</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-bc22-fd2508d83e95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constraint argument</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-b92a-c26b5bbfb114" class="">Each invariant stores:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-a7a5-f418e3cc2be7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{support}(I)=\{(s_j,\ \tau(s_j),\ \mathrm{hash}(s_j),\ \mathrm{scope}(s_j))\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8cb8-e39dc3e86fcb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-bcd0-ffba5250a2c3" class="">12) Termination classifier (Valid / Bounded / Invalid)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-af49-ea445e0d2155" class="">12.1 Valid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a743-ff6057e7ff79" class="">All gates pass, no load-bearing Limit, tests pass across declared transforms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-996d-fd5c8c2a496c" class="">12.2 Bounded</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-a213-d50215b8307c" class="">Any of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-bfb8-cfce4b43952f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">missing observables for some variables (unidentifiable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-948d-c6459fa5204c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">some transforms untestable (scope-limited)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-a634-d8bfcad7cbde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">requires external measurement not present</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b2aa-fa06e54fb92a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">consciousness closure partially specified</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a6d2-e485aeb7f14a" class="">12.3 Invalid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-bf73-c9846239d66d" class="">Any of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-86df-e194e257a6d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">contradiction under transforms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9d40-f0b4ded03468" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">untyped load-bearing claim</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-b068-d1f060320e2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">relies on Limit-typed claim to conclude hard result</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b8b8-e48b96d661a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">violates budgets (Landauer/memory) while claiming unlimited depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-9755-db60dccca56b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">non-transportable under ontology refinement</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b3eb-f4bd5dc37a4a" class="">Output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-bdd2-c33d081a43d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\text{termination}=\langle \text{class},\ \text{reasons},\ \text{failed gates},\ \text{minimal fixes}\rangle
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-9b93-f382f9465704"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-94c0-c67fb5d73670" class="">What you asked next: “More”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-9658-cf587ab3c203" class="">Here are the <strong>next missing kernel modules</strong> required to make this executable and truly gap-closed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a79f-f6979e08cf69" class="">A) Variable Registry (SSOT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-9146-e0c61e13b035" class="">A single registry that defines every variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-bb2a-eb98e9b21f76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">name, units, domain, layer, allowed transforms, observability, dependencies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-91e9-d5f73fa219e4" class="">B) Transform Test Harness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-bb0f-e8a62a641c23" class="">Given  and , generate test cases:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-9f23-f89a8663123a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Test}(I)=\{\ (x,T):\ \phi_I(x)=0 \Rightarrow \phi_I(Tx)=0\ \}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8c70-d37e8b75e3ca" class="">C) Cross-species Codebook</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-bc34-fad68e6b66ec" class="">A mapping between homologous variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8a04-e50ad7c54861" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma:\ (v,\text{species}_a)\mapsto (v&#x27;,\text{species}_b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8637-e26c2a71b437" class="">D) EM Layer Formalization</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-947f-c60af52e3c1c" class="">Explicit channel model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a4db-e679ea3ec3d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C=B\log_2(1+\mathrm{SNR})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-a0eb-fbbf4749e5f4" class="">E) Invariant Search Procedure</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-92f9-efffc75156b9" class="">A deterministic search over operator-generated candidates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b773-f278605df55c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_{t+1} = \mathrm{TopK}\big(\mathrm{Score}(I)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-98d4-c442f2cba7e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8c92-e835237e7807" class="">If you want the next output immediately</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-8b2d-d245bc9f979a" class="">Pick one word and I will output it fully as a spec (same rigor as above):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-bd8a-c0dd1e39a252" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>REGISTRY</strong> (full variable registry template + required fields + examples across physics/bio/cog/EM/soc/cosmic)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-b890-ddecb403ae52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>TRANSFORMS</strong> (full transform library + test harness design + deterministic generators)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-ab6f-c22dca4e38f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CODEBOOK</strong> (cross-species mapping formalism + scaling laws + validation gates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9c61-da19894d3b7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SEARCH</strong> (deterministic invariant discovery algorithm + scoring + pruning + proof obligations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-ae5c-c96920ee771b" class="">Mapping the Minimal Consciousness Kernel (C1–C5) to Biology</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-822a-e546e0b238ce" class="">Scope: map each condition to concrete biological substrates and measurable proxies (human + animal). No metaphors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-ad6b-c465e5cee0b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-b00c-f3d31548ef8f" class="">C1 — Persistent Identity → “Stable Self-State”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-beff-c6cbee03ac00" class="">Biological substrates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-be94-cd65afb6af54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Default Mode Network (DMN)</strong>: medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC), posterior cingulate cortex (PCC)/precuneus, angular gyrus.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-b386-d58cf7fefb7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hippocampus + medial temporal lobe (MTL)</strong>: continuity across days via episodic indexing.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8d92-d59127f5b80b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Thalamocortical loops</strong>: stability of global state across time.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-a0c2-ea49428c8ef0" class="">Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a84b-fba7de166712" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Identity is not a static object. It is a <strong>stable attractor</strong> in network state space, maintained by recurrent activity and memory consolidation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b4eb-d05b7cf081c3" class="">Observable proxies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9be2-ebeb1e0f7614" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DMN coherence/connectivity (fMRI resting-state; EEG alpha networks as proxy).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-97f0-fcb8432c133c" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Autobiographical memory stability (behavioral tests; hippocampal integrity).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9b2b-df21ee39323e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Identity persistence under perturbation (sleep deprivation, anesthesia emergence, dissociation).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-b294-d838b7942518"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-bf26-f6aab457d5c0" class="">C2 — Recursive Self-Model (Depth ≥ 2) → “Self-model + Meta-model”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9c8c-c1af76f3cfe4" class="">Biological substrates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9d7f-d8ad1133b839" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>mPFC</strong>: self-referential modeling.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-b623-de38de3e5ecb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Anterior insula + anterior cingulate cortex (ACC)</strong>: interoceptive model + error monitoring.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-ae46-e3a87601ba93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Temporoparietal junction (TPJ)</strong>: perspective taking, self/other separation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-af5b-c5be2272fe94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dorsolateral prefrontal cortex (dlPFC)</strong>: meta-cognitive control, explicit re-representation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b4f6-d2ca9dd25833" class="">Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8906-c0b43020ee6e" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Level 1: world model (sensory → prediction).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-89f6-ff800232dc4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Level 2: model of <em>the system doing the modeling</em> (confidence, error, ownership, agency).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-ba2d-ff69f0e2b49f" class="">Observable proxies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-90ed-e630b960881f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Metacognitive accuracy (confidence calibration vs performance; type-2 ROC).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-9e97-d7804e3b1d14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Error awareness signatures (ERN/Pe in EEG; ACC activation).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-8f86-f9879f274799" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agency attribution tasks (intentional binding; sensory attenuation of self-generated touch).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-a1f8-fe4d205794d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mirror self-recognition (partial proxy; not sufficient alone).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9d03-dd91c81a4cf1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8acc-ce0257aeb970" class="">C3 — Error-Corrected Continuity → “Repair, Not Just Memory”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-9bd9-f4a49d1e982c" class="">Biological substrates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-955f-ccdcfdd0a9a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Noradrenergic locus coeruleus (LC)</strong>: g
ain control; stabilizes/updates representations.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-962d-ed818e8a5637" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dopamine systems (VTA → striatum/PFC)</strong>: prediction error learning; policy update.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-a046-e78dd642e717" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Sleep architecture</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-b130-fc64f25dcd3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">NREM: synaptic renormalization + memory consolidation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-b97f-cc06180efb62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">REM: integration/association + affective processing</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8be6-e3cf5f19f55c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Glia + homeostatic plasticity</strong>: long-timescale stabilization of circuits.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-ad37-feb2066f6913" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Immune–brain interface (microglia, cytokines)</strong>: can destabilize continuity when inflamed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-9296-f42f08f36dbd" class="">Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9168-d2053d653924" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Continuity requires <strong>active maintenance</strong> (reconsolidation, homeostatic plasticity, sleep-dependent repair).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8cca-f8b550c7ed20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Failure modes: 
elirium, dementia, dissociation, psychosis—each is continuity repair failure in different subsystems.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9d1d-f1093df41244" class="">Observable proxies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8bf0-e0c61d40e534" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sleep metrics (spindles, slow-wave activity; REM density).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-84a0-f1a176ba6265" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prediction error dynamics (dopamine-sensitive tasks; reinforcement learning parameters).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-a1d6-c31570cdc663" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cognitive fluctuation indices (reaction time variability; delirium screening).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-8cdf-c1d7aee4dcc2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Inflammation markers correlated with cognitive stability (CRP, cytokines; context-dependent).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b158-e843322b1892"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9baa-d2ee4a15645d" class="">C4 — Embodied Closed Loop → “Sensorimotor + Interoceptive Control”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-8404-c98c453d4c79" class="">Biological substrates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-85b5-c84025558c4e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Brainstem (PAG, reticular formation)</strong>: arousal and survival control loops.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-bcec-d1e4c126abff" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hypothalamus</strong>: homeostatic regulation (temperature, hunger, thirst).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-8955-c125817ee9bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Insula</strong>: interoception (internal body state representation).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-9df7-e8f28bb5a27d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cerebellum</strong>: predictive control, error correction for actions (including cognitive timing).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a940-e1e665662036" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Basal ganglia</strong>: action selection, habit vs goal arbitration.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a68b-f138d73d3c08" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Somatosensory + motor cortex</strong>: body schema and action execution.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-99d1-df6be6b45d81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Vagus nerve + autonomic system</strong>: bidirectional body–brain regulation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8bd4-eb8039911d9d" class="">Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9c84-cf7b5d0e6500" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Consciousness requires the system to be <strong>causally embedded</strong>: actions change inputs; internal state changes behavior; body constraints shape the state-space.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-bd82-c12170702857" class="">Observable proxies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-abc6-cf6db83e8592" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interoceptive accuracy (heartbeat detection/ discrimination tasks).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-bc14-f1e13dc5a34f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Autonomic coupling (HRV, baroreflex sensitivity; context-specific).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-886b-d491d163db98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sensorimotor prediction (cerebellar tasks; mismatch negativity).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-919d-e8708a62a6fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agency measures (action–outcome contingency learning).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-ac2a-d73efc9bf625"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-967b-c9f11e2eb781" class="">C5 — Finite-Budget Stability → “Resource Constraints and Control Bandwidth”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-be8e-c941e10b2a96" class="">Biological substrates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-bd8b-c6466191c4b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Energy supply</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-a6f1-cb4b0e18f0fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">glucose/oxygen delivery</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-b6ec-e0c0a95c10da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">mitochondrial function</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b65a-eab5e707faa8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">neurovascular coupling</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-b90e-e1d2d86f2f0f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Neuromodulators as budget allocators</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b7d0-e1067e7bc18e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">norepinephrine (LC): exploration vs stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-8bcc-cc28e8d6d6a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">acetylcholine: sensory precision vs priors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-9768-cf5d81e4889b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">dopamine: learning/update allocation</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-9fef-cb095d52097a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Thalamus</strong>: bandwidth gate for cortical access.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-ad66-e6b2e2673c92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>PFC</strong>: executive budget allocation (attention, working memory).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-af7a-f2da9f28687d" class="">Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-af4a-e31806bb6503" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Conscious function occurs under strict limits: metabolic power, working memory capacity, synaptic update costs, and delays in feedback loops.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-af31-ff5f04ffd22d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Depth increases cost; cost imposes ceilings.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9474-ff50aa329331" class="">Observable proxies</h2></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-a5a2-e23b1df89ea6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cognitive load capacity (n-back/complex span; working memory limits).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-aad8-dc66f0f01a89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pupillometry (LC activity proxy; effort allocation).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-8d58-cc01d7840f3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EEG markers of arousal/resource state (theta/beta ratios; context-dependent).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-ae28-c8759aaa98ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Perfusion/metabolism measures (fNIRS, PET; specialized).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-bff6-c23cc6cc5b02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-acc9-e2132cd40789" class="">Species Mapping (Cross-Species, Non-verbal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ac84-e8006d7b7da3" class="">What to test (species-agnostic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-a1d1-c5924f0daf7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C1: persistence of an internal state across time (stable preferences, consistent policy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-aa92-dbec98a816b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C2: meta-level uncertainty monitoring (confidence-like behavior; opt-out paradigms)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a800-ffef30768330" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C3: recovery after perturbation (sleep-like states, reconsolidation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-a4a2-def4726099cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C4: closed-loop control (active sensing, goal-directed behavior)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9855-c33c7942962b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C5: budget-limited tradeoffs (effort discounting, fatigue dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-a35a-c12ef7a55a20" class="">Practical examples</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9841-e9c616377e0d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Corvids/primates: strong evidence for C2 via uncertainty monitoring tasks.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-8ce5-f451ea87ba1f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Octopus: strong C4; distributed control; partial C1/C3 evidence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-9ef7-e0b406da801e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rodents: C3/C4 well-studied; C2 debated but measurable with opt-out tasks.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8e7b-d186162b24e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Insects: strong C4, limited C2 evidence; likely shallow depth but not zero.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b830-ce2d6f472443"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b5af-f32808a0e3fe" class="">One-line mapping table (no tables, just mapping)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8038-ed5b06809d1d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Identity (C1)</strong> → DMN + hippocampus + thalamocortical stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
