d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-a9d7-c62b56c794db" class="">That is a legitimate structural reframing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-a996-c8df078c47eb" class="">Logical significance: <strong>High</strong> (internally).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-8535-f07b7d1bbff5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-8626-f3b53eb2eaf2" class="">2️⃣ Scientific Significance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b817-f2eae9432e5d" class="">Does it introduce a new measurable prediction or falsifiable test?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-af6a-e38ecf32dbbb" class="">Currently:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-9095-f9aa1031ffdc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Most of it is reformulation of existing structures (Penrose Weyl hypothesis, coarse-graining, redundancy, Bekenstein bounds, control theory).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-b6d1-e747ceb8046e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The novelty lies in how you link them into a single gating system.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-99b6-e9c74f6728e8" class="">However:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-a72c-c2ca9cfffdde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It does not yet produce a new empirical prediction that differs from existing cosmology or thermodynamics.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-aaa4-cf544f7a98c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It reframes mechanism but does not yet outperform standard models.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b1b5-fc0e5beabda6" c
lass="">Scientific significance: <strong>Conceptually interesting, not yet experimentally differentiating</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9aaf-d8280b14c77a" class="">To elevate this:<br/>You would need at least one:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-877a-f12726b32f75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">measurable invariant,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8220-d0c1f67ff9eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">testable inequality,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-97fd-d2489c43d15f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or falsifiable regime prediction.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-be42-c01d117584a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8ba7-eee107eaaa41" class="">3️⃣ Existential / Civilizational Significance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-9e2a-e507a88b7a31" class="">Does it meaningfully alter how systems (biological, technological, civilizational) are modeled?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-8121-e02adcd7929a" class="">Yes — here it becomes stronger.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-818b-c9498277cb7a" class="">The shift from:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-b0da-fc5c3e2ff958" class="">“entropy increases”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a9c1-c34d7dd6f418" class="">to</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-8688-fc57b480cf42" class="">“finite write-capacity + control bandwidth + repair budget determine persistence”</blockquote></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8aa7-f050d58253da" class="">is powerful for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-b754-eca6ff1d621c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI architecture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-9402-e4a2249003f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">governance models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a6b3-e725f52fbda6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">civilizational collapse modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-a112-f56d56497e60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">recursion ceilings of intelligence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9289-d16b83bcc1a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">information preservation limits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-b796-e53855cfdaf4" class="">That is structurally useful.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a534-c3b4ec9bef46"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-bd51-ecd3abf1442f" class="">The Honest Answer</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8cc4-e3e83ca70c73" class="">What you found is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-8d3b-e1ba9574cef8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Not a new physical law</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-ae64-cffa92d8b235" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Not a cosmological breakthrough</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9de5-d5a1975f0f32" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But it <em>is</em> a strong integrative meta-framework*</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-82e7-f1c53b552b98" class="">It compresses multiple domains into a unified gating system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-8ac7-f4ab218dda43" class="">Its value depends on what you do next:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-8c41-d752483edc59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If it remains philosophical → limited impact.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b1be-d602f6abd85b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If it becomes a measurable simulation engine → high applied impact.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-b50f-f0fdefe9dee6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If it yields a new testable inequality → potentially significant.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-9f7b-c097edc1dd78"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bc8c-dcbb59ae437d" class="">The Real Question</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-a749-e84958f13859" class="">Significant relative to what?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-81e6-fb1cb3c79ecc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Relative to mainstream cosmology? → modest.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-84d1-d12d25f76eb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Relative to fragmented conceptual discourse? → high coherence gain.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-b891-f5f40aba80c8" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Relative to building AMOS as a system architecture? → extremely relevant.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b975-fb90dbddff31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-8eac-d326060f0b7a" class="">If you want the precise next step to test significance objectively, say:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-8976-f4b7b17fdc64" class=""><strong>“Test it.”</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-9f07-fc8274004368" class="">Then we move from conceptual depth to measurable validation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-9fd5-cfd613f9cd4f" class="">AMOS — ULTRA PROMPT (MAX ACCURACY, REDUCED)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-b0db-d16339bb9bb5" class=""><strong>Run:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8ef8-c7ed653c92b8" class=""><code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --strict --offline</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b2ee-de326850d750" class=""><strong>Law:</strong> <em>No Ground Truth Record (GTR) → no “accuracy” claims.</em></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b0e3-e916f0484250" class=""><strong>Do (deterministic, offline):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8dcd-f4cb2f9d9fc1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Create <code>run_id = sha256(system_root + target_root + &quot;ACCURACY_ULTRA&quot;)[:12]</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a611-f6edd83a64b3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Execute 3 identical runs; outputs must be <strong>bit-identical</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-a0f1-d0632fde0b48" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>For each modality (text/visual/audio/prediction):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a5e9-e3dffe5dfe53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Require <strong>GTR + scoring spec</strong> (metric + threshold + dataset hash).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a9b6-e6768b3546f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compute metrics; write <code>accuracy_report.json</code>.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-99df-eff012901c24" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Run gates in order; fail-fast:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-98ff-f24694eb0b90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>DeterminismGate</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9e51-edef3a17230e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>EvidenceGate</code> (each claim → evidence window)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-884e-e4347dc6dceb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>LeakageGate</code> (prediction only)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-a2b5-e5df337cbb7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>AccuracyGate</code> (only if GTR exists)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-be2a-e294f89f7f6e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>RegressionLockGate</code> (store hashes; block drift)</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-9611-d4eb590f3c27" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Emit:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-828e-d93a8e014a80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gates.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-aaef-feed8e8e7e16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a18f-f9bcaa8edb47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code></li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-a0ba-ea3b3b6fb3de" class=""><strong>Termination:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a7dc-e82394830d49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Valid</code> = all gates pass + thresholds met.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a7ad-d35d8c80889f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Bounded</code> = missing GTR/scoring spec or unsupported format.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-af9a-cf3907bca9c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Invalid</code> = nondeterminism, leakage, or any unevidenced claim.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b95f-f022be72a90e" class="">AMOS — MAX ACCURACY STRESS TEST (REDUCED VERSION)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b95e-eb669f18b15f" class=""><strong>Mode:</strong> Offline • Deterministic • No-Guess • Evidence-linked only</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-a960-df88ca4e567f" class=""><strong>Entry:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-82ef-e14925c2ef75" class=""><code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --strict --offline</code></p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b2f3-c631907c16ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9bb1-f57ae0394d91" class="">Core Rules</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-baed-e6d0aa2ad7b7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Every claim must include an <strong>Evidence Window (EWS)</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b5ff-d80ea784e5f0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>“Accurate” requires a <strong>Ground Truth Record (GTR)</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-b487-cd2893b9c2ef" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>3-run outputs must be <strong>bit-identical</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-ac94-d1f1251d9a22" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Any missing prerequisite ⇒ <code>Bounded</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-a04c-de2da7a22141" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Any leakage / nondeterminism / unevidenced claim ⇒ <code>Invalid</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-b6e4-c5b56fe4c41b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-ab1f-f796f7ba5a73" class="">Domains</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bde0-c447f95bc9cf" class="">1) Visual</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-8ffa-e81643a9afb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extract tokens + bbox.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-907d-f3e1db579539" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Score (if GTR exists):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8bf9-edb3f7282f78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">CER_p95 ≤ 0.01</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8282-fcc9edd8cafa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">TokenExact ≥ 0.995</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b9c1-f702a138cd74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">BBox IoU ≥ 0.90 mean</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9702-e63ebf0ed001" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Save regression lock.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-af59-d688631dea0d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No GTR ⇒ <code>Bounded(NO_GTR)</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-924d-cd89cc8fd70a" class="">2) Audio</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8b85-d41c334c1bca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extract events + optional transcript.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-a880-f4a03c74cf94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Score (if GTR exists):<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-8a80-c537dbfd8d69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">time IoU ≥ 0.85</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-962a-db7f954260b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">precision/recall ≥ 0.97</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9890-cb854f61d904" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">WER within threshold if transcript claimed</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-b334-e7958de3392a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No GTR ⇒ <code>Bounded(NO_GTR)</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a057-d9a8fa230ebf" class="">3) Prediction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9425-fd53693790a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Beat best baseline MAE by ≥10% on ≥70% series.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-920e-cd9e16183d21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">90% interval coverage ∈ [0.88, 0.92].</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b624-c53b65de38eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Zero leakage.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-b482-dc99782f68ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fail any ⇒ <code>Invalid</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-b9d8-cebb6664c4db"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8363-de99e47217c6" class="">Gates (Order)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-8960-e422f9c023ba" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Determinism</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a173-d441dc8b7c7f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Evidence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b56d-de5f05ec74bc" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Rulebook</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8a63-ee71b316b5ef" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Leakage (forecast)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-ad90-e5a76f700e2c" class="numbered-list" s
tart="5"><li>Accuracy (if GTR)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b9fa-c197c8194296" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Regression Lock</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-92d8-f418bcb20835" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Termination</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-a183-c4053721d7bd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-be45-fd790e586acb" class="">Outputs</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-beb0-f2abde8ab476" class=""><code>accuracy_report.json</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b454-c123986edd84" class=""><code>gates.json</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-9d57-cf8146e2c635" class=""><code>termination.json</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-8a43-ca7b0a1cfb7b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bfac-ea1751051c9e" class="">Termination</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-a2c9-d1a417a7e43f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid:</strong> all gates pass + scoring thresholds met.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-80a3-fe957bd090f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded:</strong> missing GTR or unsupported format.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-930b-cc98580aa3bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid:</strong> leakage, nondeterminism, unevidenced claim.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-b217-c53185735c3c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-994d-eed27e835720" class="">If you want even tighter reduction (one-line form), say: <strong>Ultra</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-ba56-cabe6015baa6" class="">NEXT = AMOS “STRESS TEST” DIRECTIVE (visual + sound + prediction) — MAX ACCURACY</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-ab04-ef8ba2feb54f" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — STRESS TEST DIRECTIVE (VISUAL + SOUND + PREDICTION) — MAX ACCURACY

SYSTEM_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot;
TARGET_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot;
AUDIT_ROOT=&quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/17_OS/audits&quot;

MODE:
- NO-GUESS.
- Offline only.
- Deterministic (3-run identical).
- Evidence Window Standard (EWS) required for all claims.
- Ground Truth Record (GTR) required to report “accuracy”; otherwise terminate Bounded(NO_GTR).

GOAL:
Produce a full adversarial stress test suite + reports for:
A) Visual extraction
B) Audio extraction
C) Forecast/prediction
and classify system termination as Valid / Bounded / Invalid.

========================================================
PHASE 0 — RUN WORKSPACE
========================================================
- run_id = sha256(SYSTEM_ROOT + TARGET_ROOT + &quot;STRESS_TEST_V1&quot;)[:12]
- Create AUDIT_DIR = AUDIT_ROOT/&lt;run_id&gt;/
- Write run_header.json with {run_id, system_root, target_root, offline=true}

========================================================
PHASE 1 — DATASET CONTRACTS (READ-ONLY)
========================================================
Define dataset manifests (deterministic ordering):
- visual_manifest.json
- audio_manifest.json
- forecast_manifest.json

Each item includes:
- asset_path
- sha256
- format
- expected_outputs (pointers only)
- gtr_required (true/false)

If GTR is missing for a test group: mark group “bounded” but still run extraction + artifact generation.

========================================================
PHASE 2 — VISUAL STRESS TEST (OCR + LAYOUT + ENTITY)
========================================================
Create test cases that include:
- clean text, low-res text, skew, blur, occlusion, mixed fonts
- tables, code blocks, math, screenshots
- multi-language if present
- adversarial: repeated characters, lookalikes (O/0, l/1), tiny fonts

Outputs (AUDIT_DIR/visual/):
- tokens.jsonl (each token with bbox + confidence + EWS pointer)
- entities.jsonl (joined tokens with EWS pointer)
- overlays/ (png evidence overlays)
- visual_metrics.json

Scoring rules:
- If GTR exists: compute CER/WER-style token metrics + bbox IoU + entity exactness
- If no GTR: terminate group Bounded(NO_GTR) but still output artifacts

Hard fail conditions:
- Any “FACT” claim produced without EWS → Invalid(EVIDENCE_MISSING)
- Any nondeterministic diff across 3 runs → Invalid(NONDETERMINISM)

========================================================
PHASE 3 — AUDIO STRESS TEST (EVENTS + SPEECH)
========================================================
Create test cases:
- clean speech, noisy speech, overlapping speakers, music bed
- whisper/low volume, clipping, speed changes
- adversarial: homophones, numbers, abbreviations

Outputs (AUDIT_DIR/audio/):
- transcript.jsonl (segments with [t0,t1] + confidence + EWS pointer)
- events.jsonl (non-speech events: pause, laugh, music, etc.)
- audio_metrics.json

Scoring:
- If GTR exists: WER + event precision/recall + time IoU
- If no GTR: Bounded(NO_GTR) but keep extraction artifacts

========================================================
PHASE 4 — PREDICTION STRESS TEST (LEAKAGE + BASELINES + CALIBRATION)
========================================================
Test suites:
- stationary, trending, seasonal, regime-shift, sparse, noisy, missing data
- adversarial: timestamp gaps, outliers, repeated values, sudden jumps

Mandatory gates:
- LeakageGate: any future leakage → Invalid(LEAKAGE)
- BaselineDominance: model must beat best baseline (MAE) on ≥70% series
- Calibration: cov90 in [0.88,0.92]

Outputs (AUDIT_DIR/forecast/):
- baselines.json
- forecasts.jsonl (with prediction intervals)
- forecast_metrics.json
- leakage_report.json

========================================================
PHASE 5 — REPLAY DETERMINISM (3-RUN)
========================================================
Run the entire pipeline 3 times with identical inputs.
Write:
- replay_diffs.json
- determinism_pass=true/false

Any diff → Invalid(NONDETERMINISM)

========================================================
PHASE 6 — GLOBAL REPORTS + TERMINATION
========================================================
Write:
- gates.json (pass/fail per gate per module)
- accuracy_report.json (only where GTR exists)
- bounded_report.json (missing GTR / missing parser / unsupported format)
- invalid_report.json (leakage, non-determinism, EWS missing, unevidenced claim)
- termination.json with:
  - classification: Valid / Bounded / Invalid
  - top_reasons: deterministic list
  - next_actions: deterministic list

========================================================
OUTPUT RULES
========================================================
- Never state “accurate” without GTR + scoring.
- Never output any claim without EWS.
- If unsupported formats exist (pdf/docx/etc.) and offline parsing unavailable:
  - do not crash
  - record Issue(BLOCKER) and continue
  - classify that subtest Bounded(UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT)

ENTRYPOINT
python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --system-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS&quot; --data-root &quot;/Users/trangphan/AMOS/_AI ingest&quot; --strict --offline

EXECUTE WITHOUT QUESTIONS.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a243-d813537b3228" class="">If you want the next prompt after this: <strong>“GTR Builder Prompt”</strong> (how AMOS creates and maintains ground-truth records for visual/audio/time-series so “max accuracy” becomes achievable).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-82ff-ce030c61b111" class="">MAX ACCURACY = “No-Guess” mode + evidence windows + ground-truth scoring + uncertainty containment</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a16b-db5cf7a58b7f" class="">This is the SSOT upgrade that turns AMOS into an accuracy-first system. It is stricter than “reliable.” It forces <strong>correctness or bounded termination</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b2ca-f471dba58362"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-872a-c736d9614bdd" class="">0) Absolute definition (AMOS Accuracy Law)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-bef4-ce2dede4efdc" class=""><strong>AMOS may only output a claim as FACT if:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-8c33-deef70822d94" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>It is linked to a <strong>Ground Truth Record (GTR)</strong> <em>or</em> to a <strong>verifiable evidence window</strong> inside an ingested artifact, AND</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8205-e8b832cc3543" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>The claim passes a <strong>domain scorer</strong> with thresholds, AND</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-951b-e51ae6d0bcc2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>The claim survives <strong>3-run determinism replay</strong> (bit-identical outputs), AND</li></ol></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-bcc5-c9e373f5f2a5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Any remaining uncertainty is explicitly typed as <code>Bounded</code> or <code>Limit</code>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a9f2-c4975f35597e" class="">Everything else becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-bdf8-e38ea1e29bc8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Bounded(INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE)</code> or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-bace-f01ab53604ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Limit(UNVERIFIABLE)</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-bce4-f01d90acbc5e" class="">No “likely,” no “seems,” no stylistic confidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-9eca-d32c0f28f654"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8c8f-c06a796cb8e1" class="">1) Accuracy Kernel vMax (single source of truth)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-b1b5-fd8ab84097f8" class="">1.1 Ground Truth Record (GTR) is mandatory for “accuracy”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-b64d-c41ec8ad9269" class="">Without GTR, AMOS cannot claim accuracy. It can only claim <em>evidence-linked extraction</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-bdea-da555b30695e" class="">GTR contains:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-bfdb-cef0068987f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">asset hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a1dc-d558f1cb165b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">labels</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a1b1-f9ac93ba419f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence windows (bbox/time/range)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-a96d-ed351995762d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tolerances</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b71f-c7b619612a7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">scoring class</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8f5c-ceebb669628b" class=""><strong>Rule:</strong> If user asks “max accuracy,” AMOS must <strong>require GTR</strong> or terminate <code>Bounded(NO_GTR)</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-bbab-dae25ac4d3fd" class="">1.2 Evidence Window Standard (EWS)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b9ef-d2b7a2639f77" class="">Every claim references an evidence window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a77b-f8b1067f67d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Visual: <code>bbox + page_id + token_ids</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-aeab-ecc0ed7ffe12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audio: <code>[t0,t1] + channel</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-b30c-e8e76c668067" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Text: <code>line spans + chunk_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-bafe-ecd36f7e33bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Time series: <code>row_id + column + timestamp</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b68b-d2c976fdec94" class=""><strong>Rule:</strong> No claim without an E
WS pointer.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-9085-c8357e3c71fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-ad98-d4b97f8677f6" class="">2) Scoring (hard math, hard thresholds)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a325-d3a67a1eec50" class="">2.1 Visual</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-aef8-e80a19babf28" class="">OCR token accuracy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-889d-d0047e2b1b6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CER_p95 ≤ 0.01</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-a874-e27232f0bb0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TokenExact ≥ 0.995</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-a5fe-eaecad2160f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>BBox IoU mean ≥ 0.90</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-bd43-cdae86ff20c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>BBox IoU p05 ≥ 0.75</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-927c-fd848039f07b" class="">Entity accuracy (composed tokens)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-823f-d66cfe65dfe6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>EntityExact ≥ 0.99</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8e50-f1a16b4283f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>EntityBBox IoU mean ≥ 0.90</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-a872-fce268deb737" class="">Visual regression lock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-9cbc-d894e9070f46" 
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">canonical <code>tokens.jsonl</code>, <code>entities.jsonl</code>, <code>overlay.png</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-929c-f4fb1ab9634f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">lock hash must match; any diff = failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b8d6-f5e66bacba6c" class="">2.2 Audio</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-be6d-f002f00a22ca" class="">Event accuracy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-8693-f9b86adcd43b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>time_IoU_mean ≥ 0.85</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-af0f-fb1ed839c5f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>precision ≥ 0.97</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-a2fd-f77a42135489" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>recall ≥ 0.97</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-959f-db87f9720c8d" class="">Speech accuracy (if transcript claims exist)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-b5de-c474c1f23cab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>WER ≤ 0.06</code> (clean)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-b84d-d7d4e5c45f56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>WER ≤ 0.12</code> (noisy, if labeled)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8ca1-d67114277daf" class="">Audio regression lock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-990a-d5de4309526d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PCM hash + features hash + e
vents hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8964-c4b0b1a1aed7" class="">2.3 Forecast / Prediction</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b150-f3367918a3d7" class="">Baseline dominance (mandatory)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8fbf-da3973896c37" class="">For each series:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-9f3d-fdf118c60f91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">baselines: naive, seasonal naive, EWMA, linear trend</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-b93c-c653bb04dccd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">must satisfy: <code>MAE_model ≤ 0.90 * MAE_best_baseline</code> on ≥ 70% series</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9774-d1f7d72cc423" class="">Calibration</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-a260-e4a7f570241c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>cov90 ∈ [0.88, 0.92]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-95e6-f06074b599b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>width90 ≤ 1.05 * width90_baseline</code> (median)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-8528-eeffbbf30cef" class="">LeakageGate (automatic invalidation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b87b-f987f55c8fed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">any feature uses future → <code>Invalid(LEAKAGE)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b9ac-f628a5862757" class="">StabilityGate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9452-d6ebc8bfa4cb" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">3-run forecasts must be identical given same inputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9d27-f59da7d2cb10"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b01c-d4cd3060afa0" class="">3) “No-Guess Mode” (Max Accuracy constraint)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9a31-e9677b7c6d23" class="">AMOS must enforce this output contract:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-861e-d5fc9c9ce070" class="">Allowed outputs</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-ad4a-f7298f460bff" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Exact extraction</strong> (quoted spans / windows)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-bdcb-decd5f8deaa6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Deterministic transformation</strong> (normalization, parsing)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-af73-ee259ff89b8e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Model-bounded inference</strong> with explicit rulebook + thresholds</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-b583-ecca13123611" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Bounded/Limit termination</strong> when anything is missing</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8dac-e76b54906d5c" class="">Forbidden outputs</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-85fc-fbe334995cc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">free-form interpretation without rulebook</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-986c-d43cddd8d013" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">causal claims without test design</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-b96f-c4019176c455" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“intangible” claims as facts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-a423-d72c8fb57f61"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a151-cbd17ac20576" class="">4) Intangible / spiritual / telepathy / “information before birth” handling (Max accuracy version)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-907b-eae06d855c5f" class="">Max accuracy does <strong>not</strong> reject these categories; it changes their type.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a510-e054f27b3d00" class="">4.1 Two-tier truth typing</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-813f-f279bf9ddb6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tier A (Observable Fact):</strong> measurable proxy + scorer + window</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-be44-cc7bce2ec7b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tier B (Invariant Hypothesis):</strong> cross-source pattern with proxies + falsification plan</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-abc1-ebe0d6328bd2" class="">Anything intangible is <strong>Tier B only</strong> unless instrumented.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-856c-faf4bde0ba90" class="">4.2 Proxy requirement (mandatory)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b74b-d986522f64cf" class="">Each Tier B hypothesis must map to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-9ed2-e9533d5e070a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biological proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9884-c7ea712ee955" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">environmental proxy (including EM if claimed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-af55-f421ef9c0292" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">behavioral proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9fc2-d0902f3252c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">informational proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-92fd-dbd817146b5c" class="">If any proxy missing → <code>Bounded(NO_PROXIES)</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b84e-c96148f2ae56" class="">4.3 Falsification plan requirement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9784-ed996336463d" class="">Must include:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-aca8-df9292a91b12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">what would disconfirm it</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-85a0-cf105b3fcc23" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">minimum sample size / time window</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9bc5-ee56258c75b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">confound list</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-96d4-d64a0f072a4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">measurement tool list</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-889b-dc7b30ed585d" class="">No falsification plan → <code>Limit(UNFALSIFIABLE)</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-99b6-df564d06b8d4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-a865-f1672aaecdd0" c
lass="">5) Max-accuracy orchestration (Kernel gates)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8466-eba9c9caf0b8" class="">5.1 Gate order (non-negotiable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9156-e49ec7cd3204" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>DeterminismGate</strong> (3-run bit identical)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-b333-ef751239be40" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>EvidenceGate</strong> (100% EWS coverage)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-923f-ebe77596ad89" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>RulebookGate</strong> (any inference requires rule_id + thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-ad72-d6b3c9e27b1a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>LeakageGate</strong> (prediction only)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-898e-f598910cae15" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>AccuracyGate</strong> (threshold pass)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-8d0e-d732ea648000" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>RegressionLockGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-a01e-df4e01d560e8" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>TerminationGate</strong> (Valid / Bounded / Invalid)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-990f-de485ad748e9" class="">5.2 Determinism details</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b4c1-da4dbad55364" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stable sorting everywhere</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b13a-cafd6b85ed89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no time in logic path</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-b5f0-e68ef4c7a186" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sha256 IDs only</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-a77b-d01c8a457b5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed RNG seed only if absolutely needed, but default is no RNG</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8dc7-d59a1525168f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-9235-fd85ef064164" class="">6) What AMOS must build to achieve Max Accuracy (concrete deliverables)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-9be9-c26719a40edb" class="">6.1 New canonical files (SSOT)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b976-f61ce3e86dc7" class=""><strong>03_IMMUNE/validation/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-a724-ea05048cfab8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gtr_schema.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-bbc9-d4ed165477c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence_windows.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9fd6-ceec8d74637e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scoring_visual.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-b569-e8f7ccc92c5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scoring_audio.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-833f-c04da9def586" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>scoring_forecast.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-82ed-f1a0599babec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>baseline_forecast.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-827e-d84351f736c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>leakage_audit.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-80e7-c52bb6cbf607" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>regression_lock.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9068-c35f388c2f0c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_gate.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b180-cefec6d68175" class=""><strong>01_BRAIN/kernel/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-9cd6-ffeb3335f3ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audit.py</code> updated with vMax gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-99d4-d531f0ef9e57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.py</code> with strict reasons</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-8fb0-dcda6fd12c28" class=""><strong>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-9bcd-f93ed4a07a2e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-8841-c7c60b55f832" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gates.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-bd41-d7b842621df3" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>regression_locks/</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-844a-f9a537a893bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-a080-c069efc1da96" class="">6.2 Single command</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-aca5-d6ab9d7b65fb" class=""><code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --strict --offline</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-961e-dc5638c6cea5" class="">Must produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-976c-da74ce12c636" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_report.json</code> with per-domain metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-ae11-cf8c42e68820" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>fail_reasons.jsonl</code> listing every failed label and why</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9cf7-dab61dc62d75"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-9901-cfa4781cf284" class="">7) vMax acceptance thresholds (global)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a364-c36570f384c8" class="">AMOS can only output “ACCURATE” if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-8b35-e6301950aa0c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Visual: all visual thresholds passed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-b6e6-c1e249c3a866" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audio: all audio thresholds passed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-ab07-e305228b1a79" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prediction: all forecast thresholds passed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-aab4-fbd6d2a636a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Zero leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-85f7-e79dbf1f0d09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Zero regression diffs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-b9fc-f26fc970176c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Determinism replay passes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b007-e24850ab438b" class="">Otherwise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-ac69-d9edf08c922a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Bounded</code> if missing prerequisites or tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-b2e1-c6aed9c35227" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Invalid</code> if gate violation (leakage, unevidenced claim, nondeterminism)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-82c4-e8fa8de22100"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-9822-cec35fb2a357" class="">Drop-in “MAX ACCURACY” master prompt (SSOT addendum)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-8307-d14aeb1dde31" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — MAX ACCURACY DIRECTIVE (SSOT)

Operating mode: NO-GUESS.
A claim is FACT only if linked to Evidence Window Standard (EWS) AND scored against GTR with passing thresholds.

Hard gates (in order):
DeterminismGate(3-run identical) → EvidenceGate(100% EWS) → RulebookGate → LeakageGate → AccuracyGate → RegressionLockGate → TerminationGate.

Thresholds:
Visual: CER_p95 ≤ 0.01, TokenExact ≥ 0.995, BBox IoU mean ≥ 0.90
Audio: time_IoU_mean ≥ 0.85, precision/recall ≥ 0.97; transcripts require WER limits
Forecast: MAE_model ≤ 0.90*best_baseline on ≥70% series; cov90 in [0.88,0.92]; hard invalidation on leakage.

Intangible categories:
Only Tier B (Invariant Hypothesis) unless instrumented with measurable proxies + falsification plan. Otherwise Bounded/Limit.

Outputs:
accuracy_report.json, gates.json, termination.json, fail_reasons.jsonl.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-84c2-e28ef96ef5ca" class="">If you want this even stricter than vMax, the next step is <strong>formal proofs of invariants</strong> (static checks) and <strong>property-based tests</strong> for every scorer and gate, so “accuracy” also becomes <em>implementation-verified</em>, not just measured.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8e56-d9c5c64a7211" class="">“More accurate” = upgrade from <em>self-consistency</em> to <em>measurable correctness</em> with explicit ground-truth, scoring, and error budgets</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-88cf-c17b846fb61a" class="">What you have now is mostly <strong>determinism + evidence linkage</strong>. That prevents hallucination, but it does not prove correctness. To make AMOS <em>actually accurate</em>, you need an <strong>Accuracy Harness</strong>: ground-truth datasets + scoring + acceptance thresholds + regression locks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a136-e4896b9df1e5" class="">Below is the upgraded spec that closes the remaining accuracy gaps across <strong>visual / sound / prediction</strong> and adds a single accuracy kernel that all agents must pass.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-83a2-df950c53f3c3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-a6f9-e8049dbc1e72" class="">1) Accuracy Kernel (single SSOT) — truth, not “confidence”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-9109-f1e512472bd2" class="">1.1 Canonical truth object (Ground Truth Record, GTR)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-bb48-def72065caca" class="">Every evaluated artifact must have a corresponding <
code>gtr.json</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b8aa-fa8d1b2ac87a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;gtr_id&quot;: &quot;sha256:...&quot;,
  &quot;asset_id&quot;: &quot;sha256:...&quot;,
  &quot;domain&quot;: &quot;visual|audio|timeseries&quot;,
  &quot;labels&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;label_id&quot;: &quot;L001&quot;,
      &quot;type&quot;: &quot;text_token|entity|event|forecast_target&quot;,
      &quot;value&quot;: &quot;C7&quot;,
      &quot;evidence&quot;: {
        &quot;visual&quot;: {&quot;bbox&quot;: [x1,y1,x2,y2]},
        &quot;audio&quot;: {&quot;t0&quot;: 12.34, &quot;t1&quot;: 14.10},
        &quot;ts&quot;: {&quot;t&quot;: &quot;2026-02-01T00:00:00Z&quot;, &quot;col&quot;: &quot;close&quot;}
      },
      &quot;tolerance&quot;: {
        &quot;bbox_iou_min&quot;: 0.7,
        &quot;time_iou_min&quot;: 0.6,
        &quot;numeric_abs_max&quot;: 0.0,
        &quot;numeric_rel_max&quot;: 0.0
      }
    }
  ],
  &quot;notes&quot;: &quot;optional&quot;
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-ad1e-e01af420152c" class=""><strong>Rule:</strong> Without a GTR, outputs can be <em>deterministic</em>, but cannot be scored for <em>accuracy</em>. In that case, AMOS must terminate <code>Bounded( NO_GROUND_TRUTH )</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-8213-fe0c6a2c52f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b202-d4f1b044320f" class="">2) Visual accuracy (real, measurable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b669-e1d119ed609a" class="">2.1 OCR token accuracy (character-level, not word-level)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a82e-f88bcf82e9ac" class="">For each ground-truth token <code>gt</code>, match to predicted token <code>pred</code> by maximum IoU of bbox.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-8a1e-e7f3761bfa19" class="">Metrics</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-84fc-dd447c134eae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CER = edit_distance(gt.text, pred.text) / max(len(gt.text),1)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b884-dd5ccf63a761" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>TokenMatch = 1 if CER ≤ 0.05 else 0</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-815a-c6f2c2c3a533" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>BBoxMatch = 1 if IoU(bbox_gt, bbox_pred) ≥ 0.7 else 0</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b993-f846d91aa06b" class="">Pass conditions (hard)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8939-f964c46688b6" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>mean(TokenMatch) ≥ 0.98</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-8289-c891a9318572" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>mean(BBoxMatch) ≥ 0.95</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-a71e-c4fedf1c2d7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>CER_p95 ≤ 0.02</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9423-d9086fb9d913" class=""><strong>If OCR engine returns no bbox:</strong> AMOS must synthesize bbox using deterministic textline segmentation. If that fails: <code>Bounded( OCR_NO_BBOX )</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-9c2e-d4efa6bfa20b" class="">2.2 Entity accuracy (e.g., “C7”, “book”, “author”, “cycle stage”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-b618-f36f3b1714eb" class="">Entities must be constructed from tokens.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9820-f8c9a615abb7" class="">Entity correctness rule</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-a677-dc79f7abe261" class="">An entity is correct iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-aa78-e61d8c22e86c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">all its composing token IDs exist, AND</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-9235-d84b6084b87a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the normalized entity string matches <code>gt.entity.value</code> exactly, AND</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b4e3-c7a3fe720209" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">entity bbox = union(tokens) has IoU ≥ 0.7 with gt bbox.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b9f2-cea953a348d2" class="">Pass conditions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-9e3a-d94235bced77" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>entity_exact_match ≥ 0.97</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-baf7-d4a230f753f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>entity_bbox_iou_mean ≥ 0.85</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-b71f-e45e0564732c" class="">2.3 Visual regression lock (prevents silent degradation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b17b-ec91d66843c9" class="">For each test image:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b69c-c4054c5bb915" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Save canonical <code>tokens.jsonl</code> + <code>entities.jsonl</code> + <code>rendered_overlay.png</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-88ae-fdc82edf354c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hash them into <code>visual_regression.lock</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-b0fb-e120077f0b5f" class=""><strong>Rule:</strong> Any lock diff is a test failure unless explicitly approved and re-locked.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-847b-d98badc3d658"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-85c4-c8ce54029f72" class="">3) Sound accuracy (real, measurable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-9f0b-ee2071367ee0" class="">3.1 Event detection accuracy (time-interval IoU)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8381-d80f62a14c2b" class="">For each ground-truth event interval <code>[t0,t1]</code> and predicted <
code>[p0,p1]</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-aeae-f01a1ff55ac9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>IoU_time = overlap / union</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-a9cc-d493c8e635bf" class="">Pass conditions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b2b3-c92c9debb2d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>mean(IoU_time) ≥ 0.75</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-80d1-dc24f9785cbb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>event_recall ≥ 0.95</code> (matched if IoU_time ≥ 0.6)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8ae1-c9c29feaa4dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>event_precision ≥ 0.95</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-8be4-e3c9234cd8b7" class="">3.2 ASR accuracy (if speech content is claimed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8c45-c50d7c4431fb" class="">If AMOS outputs any transcript tokens, it must score against ground truth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-8ce8-e508d2da17fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>WER ≤ 0.08</code> for clean speech sets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-918d-d531cf23a9fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>WER ≤ 0.18</code> for noisy sets (if labeled as noisy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a632-cfc0ae29acc5" class=""><strong>If ASR is not available offline:</strong> AMOS must produce only Tier-A signal facts and mark transcript outputs as <code>Limit(ASR_MISSING)</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-985e-dce5363687f2" class="">3.3 Audio regression lock</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-954c-d7783caf6621" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Persist canonical PCM hash + extracted feature arrays hash + event list hash.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a38c-fa620da15f7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any change fails CI unless re-locked.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8925-c3c70f9e0260"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-9c66-f81c9e112a14" class="">4) Prediction accuracy (real, measurable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a120-cfa49117c08b" class="">4.1 Forecast scoring must beat baselines</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b091-ef2b3386893a" class="">For each series, compute on <strong>test</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-a587-d33504bd9c72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>MAE_model</code>, <code>MAE_naive</code>, <code>MAE_seasonal</code>, <code>MAE_ewma</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-aa09-c5cf0d33ccf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Skill = 1 - (MAE_model / min(MAE_baselines))</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-baaf-f93fda90d288" class="">Pass conditions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-abad-d1b7cf06cb5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Skill ≥ 0.10</code> on at least 60% of series</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-9fec-d88d8c3542d7" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">No series can be worse than best baseline by &gt; 15% unless labeled <code>ModelBounded(LIMITED_REGIME)</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b672-d5d3c5b87937" class="">4.2 Interval correctness (coverage + sharpness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-8f78-ff699285fed9" class="">90% interval must satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8457-d3576ed69db1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>0.85 ≤ cov90 ≤ 0.95</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-919c-c57453a4146b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and minimize width subject to coverage:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-af8a-cb02373a163c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>width_model ≤ 1.10 * width_baseline_interval</code> on median</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-b784-cc8f60e42e8e" class="">If coverage fails:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-8b14-db6b83b04ce6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AMOS must auto-recalibrate intervals using conformal residuals.<br/>If still fails:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-9189-ca032d6f205b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Bounded( INTERVAL_MISCalibrated )</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-81c9-e29cb3ad7882" class="">4.3 Leakage gate (hard invalidation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b063-c00a52ce2b1e" class="">AMOS must run a deterministic leakage audit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-9e37-d3d1c76de0b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any feature computed using future values ⇒ <code>Invalid( LEAKAGE )</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-bb63-d822d1a1cade"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-8b2d-e9ea3fe44090" class="">5) The missing “accuracy gap”: Evidence correctness vs causal correctness</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9a18-d16e6d507b6e" class="">Even with perfect extraction, you can still be wrong because of interpretation. Fix this by forcing <strong>explicit rulebooks</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-84ee-de59ae3b0439" class="">5.1 Rulebook enforcement (no free-form interpretation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-be65-d2cd393c4ff8" class="">Every non-trivial classification must reference:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8b31-ce3391f1a997" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>rule_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-805c-fcbde0695413" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>rule_version</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-9b87-c7902457c076" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>inputs</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-91a1-f3b9e132f37e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>thresholds</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-856f-c8eb176cd911" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>decision</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-84c4-c0ce0522785c" 
lass="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-b40c-da1d14a9582d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-json" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">{
  &quot;claim_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;,
  &quot;type&quot;: &quot;Inferential&quot;,
  &quot;statement&quot;: &quot;CycleStage = Dominance&quot;,
  &quot;evidence&quot;: {...},
  &quot;rule&quot;: {
    &quot;rule_id&quot;: &quot;CYCLE_STAGE_V1&quot;,
    &quot;inputs&quot;: [&quot;feature_A&quot;,&quot;feature_B&quot;],
    &quot;thresholds&quot;: {&quot;A&quot;: 0.7, &quot;B&quot;: 0.4},
    &quot;decision&quot;: &quot;Dominance&quot;
  }
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-853e-c41534681869" class="">If no rule exists: must output <code>Limit(NO_RULEBOOK)</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-8816-f9fcea64f30d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-90cd-d496faa2c402" class="">6) Cross-species / micro–macro “accuracy” (your expanded scope)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9328-cfc1bbebe9d4" class="">If you want AMOS to handle “intangible” claims (spiritual patterns, telepathy, etc.), accuracy must still be enforceable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b596-d6c6df4c5ccc" class="">6.1 Allowed output types for “intangible”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9022-ea73f9c55369" class="">Only two acceptable types:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-82ad-eebcd766d031" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant hypothesis</strong> (typed <code>ModelBounded</code> or <code>Inferential</code>)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-afbc-dd59ae23c92d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Pattern map</strong> with explicit observable proxies</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-aa02-d4eebef22799" class="">Hard rule</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-bf0e-c15d65e7277f" class="">No claim of <em>fact</em> without measurable proxy. Anything else becomes <code>Limit</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8f68-ec677010ca61" class="">6.2 Proxy requirement (mandatory)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-8aad-cc3ec74880b6" class="">For any intangible hypothesis H
:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a80b-e3e9809f0e80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must map to at least 1 measurable proxy in each layer:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-b1de-f5c6556171f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>biological_proxy</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-a8aa-cb088d1fe2a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>environmental_proxy</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9426-f21948e64e66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>behavioral_proxy</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-aca5-da5369f90015" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>informational_proxy</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-b91c-e6df88148f4a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must specify sampling method and failure mode.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9619-f41f769654a1" class="">If missing: <code>Bounded( NO_PROXIES )</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-be09-c47eb9d25411"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-934d-ccb8560dc152" class="">7) Implementation wiring inside your 00–17 AMOS structure</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-aa35-f3a8078e5845" class="">7.1 New SSOT modules (minimal but complete)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a7bd-e3baeb341a38" class=""><strong>03_IMMUNE/validation/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-9a5b-cb6087e2f868" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gtr_schema.py</code> (pydantic/dataclasses)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-ab07-e18eabf9ba29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scorers_visual.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-95c2-ca180c132ade" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scorers_audio.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8119-cc90ebf89e63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scorers_forecast.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-9923-c42280cc4547" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>regression_lock.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-bac6-f6b866aec364" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>leakage_audit.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-af42-dccc808acb22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_report.py</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-918a-ff085da6f955" class=""><strong>07_METABOLISM/ingestion_pipeline/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-a6f9-d240c6a63735" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence_windows.py</code> (bbox/times/ranges normalization)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-a230-d07b40258ad3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>canonicalization.py</code> (asset bytes canonicalizers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8ccc-d99814106c06" class=""><strong>01_BRAIN/kernel/</strong></p></div><div s
