tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-a89b-fbb0359ed673" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audit.py</code> must include:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-b090-cb096de02238" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">AccuracyGate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-b51d-ca716d4d7bef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">RegressionLockGate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-86ad-e13a34928c00" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">LeakageGate</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b2cc-e2580e5f6187" class=""><strong>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-bffa-dd68b58e181a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>accuracy_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-90f6-c0719388496a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>regression_diff.json</code> (if any)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-b8a0-e084c604dc7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-a948-e66a824fcc7d" class="">7.2 One command to run accuracy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-89ae-c088394a4f24" class="">Must be reachable from:<br/><code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build --strict --offline</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8d56-eb6474938813" class="">and internally call:<br/><code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master audit --accuracy</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-919a-c2f690767f1f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8392-e88f9d2a44fb" class="">8) Termination upgrade (more accurate, less ambiguity)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a0b7-d1465a71a5a6" class=""><strong>Valid</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-9774-e0d6704b02f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All gates pass AND accuracy thresholds pass for every domain that had GTR.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9956-ceb6d64cd985" class=""><strong>Bounded</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-965e-eac09d991f9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any domain missing GTR</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a554-c56cbacd6a81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any domain missing ASR/OCR but claims were correctly forced to <code>Limit</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-8936-f56aa493f493" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interval miscalibration that cannot be repaired</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-90c2-d82039137a7b" class=""><strong>Invalid</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9cff-dafc6316702f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unevidenced claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-8113-ee3d5c34a92c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9485-ffdd0ccfaadc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regression lock drift without a
pproval</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-aad1-c4e59b2c05c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-deterministic outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a207-e6bd12dd7337"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-9e4b-fdfeaba826f7" class="">9) What was missing (the exact gap list)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b1ab-ead64cc8c48a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>No ground-truth object ⇒ you can’t claim “accuracy”.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-89d9-e25b923a2aad" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>No scorer definitions ⇒ no measurable pass/fail.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-95d9-d6f81101eae5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>No regression locks ⇒ silent degradation.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-8d25-d78f835f0af3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>No leakage audit ⇒ forecasts can be fake-good.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-8551-f0bd7b6f1b38" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>No rulebooks ⇒ interpretations creep in.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-b875-f4c265fc5eb8" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>No proxy layer for intangible scope ⇒ claims become unfalsifiable.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-9e74-dc39680d8449" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>No domain-specific acceptance thresholds ⇒ “accurate” is undefined.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-927a-ec445dd9e4c4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-aca9-eb9cfbcc1b0d" class="">Drop-in “More Accurate” Prompt (SSOT addendum)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a987-d22a608e1b1e" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — ACCURACY KERNEL ADDENDUM (SSOT)

Definition:
Accuracy is only measured against GTR (ground truth records). Without GTR, outputs may be deterministic but must terminate Bounded(NO_GROUND_TRUTH).

Requirements:
- Implement GTR schema + scorers + regression locks.
- Visual: CER_p95 ≤ 0.02, token_match ≥ 0.98, bbox_match ≥ 0.95
- Audio: time IoU mean ≥ 0.75, precision/recall ≥ 0.95; ASR claims require WER thresholds
- Forecast: Skill ≥ 0.10 vs best baseline on ≥60% series; cov90 in [0.85,0.95]; hard LeakageGate

Hard gates:
- DeterminismGate (3-run identical)
- EvidenceGate (100% evidence windows)
- AccuracyGate (thresholds)
- RegressionLockGate
- LeakageGate
- RulebookGate (no interpretation without rule_id)

Termination:
Valid / Bounded / Invalid with explicit reasons.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-ab92-fe25f7df8636" class="">This is the accuracy closure layer. It replaces “more accurate” with a measurable contract that AMOS can enforce deterministically, offline, and without interpretation drift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-8652-f27c2787d0ed" class="">More accurate “close-all-gaps” spec (Visual + Sound + Prediction) — with explicit metrics, thresholds, and failure rules (Offline, Deterministic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-abec-edcf8b400214" class="">0) Global accuracy contract (applies to every subsystem)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-ae07-feb5a9cf2de1" class=""><strong>A0.1 Deterministic reproducibility</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-a72c-f1b1e77857ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Run the same command 3 times on the same inputs ⇒ <code>hash(outputs_bundle)</code> must match exactly.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a553-d0db5198ea21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>outputs_bundle</code> = sorted concatenation of all output files’ bytes (stable JSON serialization required).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-9990-c2d28617170a" class=""><strong>A0.2 Evidence correctness</strong><br/>A claim is <strong>Correct</strong> only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-9319-ef0867747b50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">It is derived from an explicit <strong>evidence window</strong> (<code>bbox</code> for visual; <code>t0,t1</code> for audio; <code>row/time range</code> for time-series), and</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8b6d-f6d1f5eb530a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The claim statement is <strong>directly entailed</strong> by extracted tokens / features within that window, or it is typed as <code>Limit</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8066-e91254df1fcf" class=""><strong>A0.3 Claim typing (UCIA-compatible)</strong><br/>Every claim must be exactly one of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-8526-eba1b1925374" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Empirical</code>: directly measurable from extracted artifacts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-8aad-fd855c70cb91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Inferential</code>: derived via explicit formula with listed assumptions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-92b0-ff340d746bf0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Definitional</code>: purely by definitions (no new facts).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-bdbd-e27b3f508728" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ModelBounded</code>: output of a model with declared validity envelope.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-81fe-d6e037c609d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Primitive</code>: declared axiom (rare; must be flagged).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-9f52-d18f4df646e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Limit</code>: “cannot conclude given constraints” (mandatory when blocked).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-baba-d995d4bdff17" class=""><strong>A0.4 No implicit i
nference</strong><br/>If the system cannot point to evidence windows and rules, it must output <code>Limit</code>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-afe9-d528a86cb78d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-b2f6-fbfb7783b033" class="">1) Visual accuracy closure (images / UI screenshots / frames)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-9e3b-f107f7af6604" class="">1.1 Output truth conditions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-a145-ffb7f25a1e1f" class="">For any extracted visual text token <code>tok</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-81ce-f38ce608a0f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tok.text</code> must be OCR output from a specific crop artifact.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-ad6d-fc8efbc39d72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tok.conf</code> must be OCR engine confidence or a deterministic proxy if engine lacks it.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-bb84-ec8df26783cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>tok.bbox</code> must be pixel coordinates on the original image.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-8876-db25852ac07b" class="">For any visual entity <code>ent</code> (e.g., “C7”, “book title”, “author”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-9928-ca9622f3804d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ent</code> must reference the exact token IDs used to build it.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9671-c880770ed215" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Normalization rules must be explicit:<div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-9664-c3ebd9bee315" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Case-folding: yes/no</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-82a5-e9c8e8467362" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Unicode normalization: NFC</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-84de-e56774959d28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Whitespace collapse: yes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-aef8-f9b5d1006c88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Punctuation stripping: only if declared</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-890b-cbd3b79a017c" class="">1.2 Minimal required operators (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-8c70-f4bec49383bb" class=""><strong>V1) Canonicalization</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a6c0-fb3a1477178d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Convert to canonical PNG bytes:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9763-c415379d6bef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>RGB</code>, no metadata, no EXIF orientation ambiguity (apply rotation if EXIF says so).</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9701-c7f5462a6bf0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>asset_id = sha256(png_bytes)[:16]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-ac40-f84d2ae331de" class=""><strong>V2) Region proposal (must run even without OCR)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b893-ea6ebd7bc624" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Produce candidate bboxes:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b14a-e0c86ce9859c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Connected-component based on adaptive thresholding (deterministic parameters).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8e5b-ed4a04870c17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Also include full-image bbox as fallback.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-afa9-d08226a22489" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If proposal fails, output one bbox = full image.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-a385-fc2c18d7835a" class=""><strong>V3) OCR</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-82b2-f0bc571b2675" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If OCR engine available: run it on each bbox crop.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a1f0-ea94a39b5459" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If OCR missing: output <code>issues(BLOCKER: OCR_MISSING)</code> and set all OCR-dependent outputs to <code>Limit</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-907b-f6782e24abf6" class=""><strong>V4) Layout grouping</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8e99-c2cfb9d197ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic grouping by y-coordinate clustering:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-bb7d-dd2e39bf3e44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Sort tokens by <code>(y_center, x_left)</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8061-f81e46e2f1c3" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:circle">Line break when <code>Δy &gt; median_height * 0.8</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9189-d08ec025370b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Block break when <code>Δy &gt; median_height * 2.0</code>.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-a8da-dbe0ad6a61a1" class="">1.3 Accuracy metrics (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9619-eb4868a14bf9" class="">If you have no ground-truth labels, “accuracy” must be <strong>self-consistency + stability</strong> metrics (not fake “precision/recall”).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-9230-f24798a3eb66" class=""><strong>V-M1 OCR Stability (same asset, 3 runs)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b555-f675d3193837" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stability = 1 - (levenshtein(text_run1, text_run2) / max_len)</code> aggregated across tokens.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-9d11-fb4230931e00" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must be <code>≥ 0.999</code> to pass DeterminismGate (because text should match exactly if deterministic; any diff is a bug).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-ae46-fae30df73582" class=""><strong>V-M2 Near-duplicate sensitivity</strong><br/>Given two images <code>A,B</code>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-9448-d45d7ab90ab5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compute perceptual hash <code>phash</code> (deterministic).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-a02e-ea4f2363527a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If <
code>phash_distance(A,B) ≤ τ_same</code> then they must be classified as “same-layout”; else “changed”.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-b268-dc5e41997465" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Set defaults: <code>τ_same = 6</code>, <code>τ_changed = 12</code>, in-between = “uncertain”.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-8116-f907cb2f45a9" class=""><strong>V-M3 Evidence integrity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-92d6-e44b2a685495" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">100% of entities must point to token IDs that exist and have bboxes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-90a1-c41b0b0dafdc" class="">1.4 Failure rules (strict)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b762-f006dfb986e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OCR present but any entity has no token evidence ⇒ <strong>Invalid</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-b5d6-c099b7065bda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OCR missing and any OCR-based claim produced ⇒ <strong>Invalid</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-89c1-c94a70de0d9d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only layout/visual diff allowed without OCR ⇒ <strong>Bounded</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-967e-dbc9c689f5b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-80a7-d5f9eaafe267" class="">2) Sound accuracy closure (audio)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a973-e01d1ffb5c88" class="">2.1 Output truth conditions</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-99f1-f01de346fd1a" class="">Audio outputs split into two tiers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-b464-fa93b77b6d1f" class=""><strong>Tier A: Signal-derived facts (always available offline)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-ae6c-d4526b8a1bf8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence segments, impulses, clipping, loudness contour, spectral centroid, periodicity proxy.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-bb46-ff33b23936ab" class=""><strong>Tier B: Speech content (ASR required)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-845e-d27b36e838b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Words, named entities, quotes → must be <code>Limit</code> without ASR.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b5f0-cea195c4590a" class="">2.2 Deterministic feature extraction (hard spec)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8769-dda7fe315e70" class="">Convert to canonical mono PCM:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b981-fd64fff56ad8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Resample to <code>16_000 Hz</code>, <code>int16</code>, mono (average channels), deterministic resampler.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-a221-ee6403cb22c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>asset_id = sha256(pcm_bytes)[:16]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-b20c-d34984f5f04f" class="">Windowing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-934c-f9025a03871d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>win = 25 ms</code>, <code>hop = 10 ms</code>, Hann w
indow.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-ba50-c95ef7dbf66e" class="">Features per window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b201-d0e3a78d50b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">RMS: <code>sqrt(mean(x^2))</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9917-c970681626ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ZCR: <code>0.5 * mean(|sign(x[n]) - sign(x[n-1])|)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-abd5-c1a5d640bf7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Spectral centroid: <code>sum(f * |X(f)|) / sum(|X(f)|)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9ebb-d07b978596b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Clipping ratio: <code>count(|x| == 32767) / N</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8539-f1a5f229cfd0" class="">2.3 Event detection thresholds (explicit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a875-ed68505f4762" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence if <code>RMS &lt; S_sil</code> for ≥ 300 ms<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b69b-f1473c880742" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>S_sil = median(RMS) * 0.1</code> (deterministic adaptive)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8ed0-d03b948ca87d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Impulse if <code>RMS(t) &gt; median(RMS) * 6</code> and duration &lt; 100 ms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-9d1f-d9ba034f9053" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Clipped if <code>clipping_ratio &gt; 0.001</code> (
0.1%)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b980-f4d43c884074" class="">2.4 Accuracy metrics (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a7d7-db6bf42f84d9" class=""><strong>A-M1 Feature determinism</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-9fa6-e08acbdf88ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">3-run max absolute diff of each feature value must be <code>0</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-bbb4-d89e105920fa" class=""><strong>A-M2 Event stability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a9c4-e438dd23be8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Jaccard overlap of detected event intervals across runs must be <code>1.0</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-a4aa-eb9080794081" class=""><strong>A-M3 ASR gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-8fa5-fc8f4273768e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If ASR exists, transcript determinism must hold (exact match bytes).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9f21-c286b6a50bd9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If ASR not deterministic, mark ASR module <strong>Invalid</strong> and degrade to Tier A only.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9942-f711708171d2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8870-e80deaffc48d" class="">3) Prediction accuracy closure (time-series / forecasting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b665-cd299e26bc7e" class="">3.1 Non-negotiable forecasting definition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9255-d5403ba82bb3" class="">A prediction is not acceptable unless it includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8e86-f2a0768cba18" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">point forecast <code>ŷ(t+h)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-ac1b-c642b7a17e2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">interval forecast <code>[L(t+h), U(t+h)]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-ba94-d0885996918a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">backtest metrics on validation window</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8dda-ff317f76c0a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">an explicit model class label (baseline vs model-bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-ba92-e34182c2eb00" class="">3.2 Deterministic split and baselines</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-86cd-ffde1a7549e2" class="">Split:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-bd24-c4cec4b35e0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Train: first 70% of time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b3c6-d42c83c854e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Val: next 15%</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b002-fad0a84c47ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Test: last 15%<br/>No shuffling.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8d0c-dfe74aaaa236" class="">Baselines (must implement all three):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-acdc-c697442398be" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Seasonal n
aive: <code>ŷ(t+h) = y(t+h-s)</code> (if seasonality known) else fallback to naive.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-a834-da994b70928b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Naive: <code>ŷ(t+h) = y(t)</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-a85c-cd038d92f922" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>EWMA: <code>m_t = α y_t + (1-α)m_{t-1}</code>, <code>ŷ(t+h)=m_t</code>, with fixed <code>α=0.2</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-951a-d0f230e8254c" class="">3.3 Deterministic intervals (mandatory)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9cd5-eb73b444c738" class="">Use residual quantiles from validation residuals:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b0ce-ebbf7b53fdff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>e = y - ŷ</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-8582-e7eeada5aace" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">For 90% interval: <code>q05 = quantile(e, 0.05)</code>, <code>q95 = quantile(e, 0.95)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-8a8b-f05c78bd81bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interval: <code>[ŷ + q05, ŷ + q95]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8b77-fa02b168916a" class="">3.4 Required metrics (accuracy must be real)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-85f1-fd464c53ccb2" class="">On validation and test:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-bf43-ecc7b8874926" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MAE, RMSE</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a037-e395ec6d8b7d" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MAPE (only if y&gt;0 else skip with Issue)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-a2fc-e99fb741b30c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interval coverage at 90%: <code>cov90 = mean(y ∈ [L,U])</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-aa6e-d50451a116e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interval width: <code>mean(U-L)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-b741-c44b48d2dc27" class=""><strong>CalibrationGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b2d2-f88792c5e5f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must have <code>0.85 ≤ cov90 ≤ 0.95</code> on validation (else prediction is <strong>Bounded</strong> with explicit “miscalibrated intervals” issue).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-93ed-cdc65f29dedf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If intervals missing ⇒ <strong>Invalid</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-9f4b-c3247edb8800"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b1a7-c57d2d88c4af" class="">4) Cross-domain stress test suite (visual + sound + prediction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a26e-fe0fd53b15de" class="">To “close all gaps,” each domain must run adversarial cases and produce explicit pass/bound/fail.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-8620-f33f0ff92e19" class="">4.1 Visual adversarial cases (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-bca9-f11aa090c192" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rotation 90/180 (apply)</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b321-cadc7db31f66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Downscale to 40% then upscale (blur)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-87d9-e2f360ed38e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">JPEG quality 30 re-encode</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-b3d3-eb72bb97b327" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">One-character difference (edit a digit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9af8-ea1c8aec8030" class=""><strong>Pass conditions</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-82fd-c85ff2b5df44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rotation handled (EXIF + manual) OR <code>Bounded</code> with reason.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9f6d-f253181d6ab6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Near-duplicate must flag change vs same appropriately (phash thresholds).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-85c1-fee575531862" class="">4.2 Audio adversarial cases (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-811a-c9679a750400" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Add 10 dB noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-80dd-c88da482bcab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mix two sources</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-ac01-e5e453c50eda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Clip artificially</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9aaa-cc09a26770da" class=""><strong>Pass conditions</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-9079-c5257f3ed161" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Event detection still stable OR <code>Bounded</code> with SNR reason.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-946a-c9a60af5808d" class="">4.3 Prediction adversarial cases (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a17c-ee30bd15fc87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Inject regime shift</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-9df0-d34f8e4ccbc5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Remove a block (missingness)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-89ba-f52382293248" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Add outlier spike</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bdd3-fc94122efc2e" class=""><strong>Pass conditions</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-98cf-c6e3263f066f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Anomaly detector flags injected anomalies with evidence ranges OR <code>Bounded</code> with declared detection limits.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-8a46-da90e7506adb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-b695-f6e4e736ede5" class="">5) “Are we missing anything?” — remaining hard gaps (explicit)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-88f8-c9732f3c207d" class="">If you want this to be truly “closed,” these are the typical hidden holes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-984f-dfd73109de92" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Unit-of-evidence mismatch</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a254-fd9441b4259a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Visual tokens extracted from cropped image but bbox stored in crop coordinates instead of original coordinates.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-90e6-c3ca5709e05e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Must store both: <code>bbox_original</code> and <code>bbox_crop</code>.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-a7d8-cb5869c452d9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Audio timebase drift</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8886-f40572f370b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Resampling introduces fractional offsets; event times must be based on canonical resampled timebase.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-ae0f-fc55eb229d90" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Time-series timezone and irregular sampling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b53e-f44b5ef1e6be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Must normalize timestamps to UTC or declare “index-time only” and disallow calendar claims.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-82da-c3da2eb5b20b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Model leakage</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-ab86-cd2bc91cf6a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any use of future points (even in normalization) invalidates metrics.<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a1a8-e08f9f00a73a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Must compute all normalizers on train only.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9274-e6c60c41540d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>“Soft” claims creeping in</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-ab36-fae13b52ea34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Phrases like “apparent”, “clearly”, “seems” must be banned in outputs unless typed as <code>Inferential</code> with explicit assumptions and thresholds.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a2ee-ed70d5eae7e5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-adf7-e1542cbf7a4c" class="">6) Updated “more accurate” master execution prompt (drop-in)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a3a6-f367586aaa68" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">AMOS — STRESS TEST MASTER (VISUAL + SOUND + PREDICTION) — ACCURACY-LOCKED

RULES:
- Offline only. Python 3.9. Deterministic outputs.
- Every claim must be evidence-linked (bbox/times/ranges) or typed as Limit.
- No OCR/ASR =&gt; no content claims. Only Tier-A signal facts allowed.
- Prediction must include calibrated intervals and backtests; otherwise Invalid.

EXECUTION:
1) Canonicalize assets (visual-&gt;PNG bytes, audio-&gt;16kHz mono PCM int16, timeseries-&gt;normalized CSV bytes).
2) Compute asset_id = sha256(canonical_bytes)[:16].
3) Run domain pipelines with explicit thresholds and write evidence windows.
4) Run adversarial suite per domain.
5) Write:
   claims.jsonl (typed), issues.jsonl (BLOCKER/MAJOR/MINOR),
   metrics.json (real metrics), audit_report.json, termination.json.

GATES:
- DeterminismGate: 3-run identical hash(outputs_bundle).
- EvidenceGate: 0 unevidenced claims.
- CalibrationGate: cov90 in [0.85, 0.95] on validation.
- RobustnessGate: adversarial pass or explicit Bounded issues.

TERMINATION:
Valid / Bounded / Invalid with top reasons and blockers.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a00f-d8b79490f2c3" class="">If you want the next step: specify which of these you want as the “accuracy anchor” for AMOS first: <strong>Visual</strong>, <strong>Sound</strong>, or <strong>Prediction</strong>. I will output the exact module/file-level kernel spec (functions + tests + report fields) for that domain in the 00–17 structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b888-d1188e995d8b" class="">Close-all-gaps specification (Visual + Sound + Prediction) for AMOS (Offline, Deterministic, Evidence-linked)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b75a-d685f86d500e" class="">Non-negotiable invariants (system-wide)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-8726-dd71c7585656" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Determinism</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-81f4-d975671cdd28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All IDs = <code>sha256(bytes)</code> (never uuid, never time-based).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-87dd-e3403e959390" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All iteration order = sorted lexicographically (paths, keys, records).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-bbed-cb8a37f08494" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All numeric ops = fixed precision policy (define rounding + dtype).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9698-c7a9c7a23a6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All randomness forbidden unless seeded via deterministic seed derived from input hash; log seed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-8d1d-d52e01172f5f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Evidence-linking</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-901e-d25101f25d32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every claim must include:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-99a6-e1c6d7f18c8d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>claim_id</code>, <code>support_type</code>, <code>source_asset_id</code>, <code>evidence_ptrs[]</code>, <code>confidence</code>, <code>limits[]</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-975b-ed1a0be41f07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>evidence_ptr</code> must be a resolvable pointer:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9736-fcf177713d9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">image: <code>{asset_id, page/frame, bbox_xywh, crop_artifact_id}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a829-d675767cb05f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">audio: <code>{asset_id, t0, t1, window_artifact_id}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-beeb-c56962c1d349" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">time-series: <code>{asset_id, row_range|time_range, feature_refs[]}</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8307-c1134110fefc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Bounded failure (no silent degradation)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-8955-e9ae196d81c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Missing capability (OCR/ASR/etc.) ⇒ <strong>BOUNDED</strong> with explicit Issue, never a partial h
allucinated output.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b83c-e5cb868f9cdb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any “unknown” must be represented explicitly (<code>UNK</code>, empty result, or bounded block).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-82a4-fef747857525" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>No side effects at import</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-b850-cf4ef3bca13e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All work inside explicit functions / CLI commands.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-b643-e9b3724d20f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-9d18-d489890cf98e" class="">A. Unified stress-test contract (SSOT outputs + schemas)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-8eb0-d2acc0699406" class="">Output directory (per run)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8db1-e72d07e5304b" class=""><code>17_OS/audits/&lt;run_id&gt;/stress/&lt;domain&gt;/</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9b7b-f7446b7ef363" class="">Required files (all domains)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a79d-ec3a255e86c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress_header.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-bc0e-e6bbd5c6b6ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>inputs.jsonl</code> (one record per asset)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-b37c-ea9e0160218c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>artifacts_manifest.jsonl</code> (every derived artifact: c
rop/window/index)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-bacc-e9294587f70f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>findings.json</code> (structured extraction results)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-b9a9-fe7c3f2d7a9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>claims.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-96b3-d3e8f30d76f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>metrics.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8b81-c8c1dcca3719" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>issues.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a4d5-d052a063e332" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audit_report.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-ba17-e536317fd91c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8a11-f149fc04a10f" class="">Canonical schemas (minimal)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-b01d-db130c36dc21" class=""><strong>issues.jsonl</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-b553-ca62edccafc5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>issue_id, severity(BLOCKER|MAJOR|MINOR), subsystem, code, message, evidence_ptrs[], remediation, deterministic_hash</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b2d6-e8e4086f31f3" class=""><strong>claims.jsonl</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b1b1-f6092ba3f54a" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc"><code>claim_id, statement, support_type(Empirical|Inferential|Definitional|ModelBounded|Primitive|Limit), confidence, evidence_ptrs[], assumptions[], failure_modes[]</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-95c2-cc89af6c93ff" class=""><strong>termination.json</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-9355-cc29e6ed5b5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>status(Valid|Bounded|Invalid), top_reasons[], blockers[], hash_of_outputs</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-aad5-f9b6891d5a40"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-81f6-fbc5f2362d04" class="">B. Capability closure map (no missing functions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-abe8-fbfe7365befa" class="">1) Visual closure (images / frames)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-aea9-c56e0b2b05a4" class=""><strong>Minimum viable offline stack</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9361-d2a8dc42c9d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Image decode: PIL/opencv (offline local).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-add2-fccc1b643dc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OCR: choose exactly one:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-88c3-d161690f561e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>tesseract</code> (system binary) OR</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-84f8-d73ed369e01d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>easyocr</code> (local model files) OR</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-8ecc-d4d9338c515a" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">bounded skip if not installed.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-8a01-fc3b44721715" class=""><strong>Hard-required operators</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-acb2-e01a49345918" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>detect_text_regions(image) -&gt; [bbox]</code> (can be empty)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-bc90-ca5e0258c93e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>ocr(image, bbox) -&gt; text, conf</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8d79-e76f89894345" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>layout_parse(ocr_tokens) -&gt; blocks/lines/tables</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-84a9-fc5d640226f6" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>entity_extract(layout) -&gt; entities[]</code> (ONLY from text + deterministic rules unless you have a local vision model)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b570-f4541b644075" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>diff(a,b) -&gt; changes[]</code> (hash-based + pixel-diff thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-9985-dfa99a3c50d1" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>diagram_graph(layout) -&gt; nodes/edges</code> (rule-based: arrows, boxes, adjacency via geometry)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-90a5-fdde6b63c1e8" class=""><strong>Failure closure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a2bf-fbf9e48aa8b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No OCR engine: <code>issues(BLOCKER:OCR_MISSING)</code> and <
code>termination=Bounded</code> for OCR-dependent outputs; other visual outputs may proceed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-8721-c54f5edc0b91" class=""><strong>Evidence closure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b262-e8f255669a7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every extracted token must include bbox.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-99ce-f28875284a34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every “entity” must reference the exact token span(s).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-a39b-eaffe3d8f0b5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b298-e0f79d740f86" class="">2) Sound closure (audio)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8306-e103de844257" class=""><strong>Minimum viable offline stack</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9b50-e1129c454edc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">WAV decode: <code>wave</code> or <code>soundfile</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8536-d772e576539c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ASR: optional offline (e.g., whisper.cpp local) else bounded.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-bf30-dcf204e515f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Event detection: purely signal-processing (always available offline).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-af0c-d2fee57db0cb" class=""><strong>Hard-required operators</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-ba48-db060b304401" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>window_audio(wav, win_ms, 
op_ms) -&gt; windows[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-80dd-c4e01c836667" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>compute_features(window) -&gt; {rms, zcr, spectral_centroid, …}</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-8145-d4ce55cd0fd9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>detect_events(features) -&gt; events[]</code> (silence, music-likeness proxy, impulse, sustained tone)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a7c4-d8a3da883831" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>keyword_spot(transcript|none) -&gt; hits[]</code> (if transcript exists)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-a483-e32c93200ea4" class=""><strong>Failure closure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-8c15-dc8c430217b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No ASR: transcript outputs become BOUNDED; event detection still Valid.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9db4-c7a7e5056fd1" class=""><strong>Evidence closure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-a8e4-e9f7218721e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Every event must include <code>{t0,t1}</code> and window artifact reference.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-ae18-c3de1b9f2631"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-9448-db7cb7f4fc52" class="">3) Prediction closure (time-series)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8a5e-da6a8ff5d4e2" class=""><strong>Minimum viable offline stack</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8a37-df1ee6fa29f4" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Parsers: CSV/JSON/JSONL.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-869e-fa8829160e0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Models: deterministic baselines only (no stochastic training as default).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-8c52-dc05c4260679" class=""><strong>Hard-required operators</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-bf26-f4a56a5df2c8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>validate_timeseries(schema) -&gt; ok|issues</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-99ce-cc14c0e26822" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>split(train/val/test) -&gt; deterministic by time</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-a4a2-d2015baa9f0d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>forecast_baseline(seasonal_naive|ewma|kalman_optional)</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8bad-fbd708943422" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>intervals(method) -&gt; [lo,hi]</code> (bootstrap forbidden unless deterministic seed)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a648-e521672cb614" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>anomaly_detect(residuals) -&gt; anomalies</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bec9-cac119441e59" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>regime_stage4(features) -&gt; {B,E,Dc,De}</code> (rule-based)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-ad50-e6c5cdd52c99" class=""><strong>Calibration closure</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a56a-ee1c266f5416" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Must compute coverage on validation and write <code>metrics.json</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-baad-f3e735cecfc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If intervals cannot be produced, prediction is <strong>Invalid</strong> (not merely bounded) because it violates declared requirement.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a066-d0bdc6694520"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a132-f9b10f4b5c61" class="">C. Red-team stress cases (must be included to “close gaps”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-80cd-d23364461459" class="">Visual adversarial set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a1c7-c628fc582b6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rotated text 90/180</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a99a-d9c5f41b6f17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Small font UI screenshots</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-966f-f7c4df7e25c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">JPEG artifacts + blur</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-9afb-e83c34134c66" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Near-duplicates differing by 1 icon / 1 digit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-9f4a-f0e6a0e8643b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Table screenshot with merged cells</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-b86e-c7abb7143a4f" class="">Audio adversarial set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-87b5-de90abda2105" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Speech + music overlay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-881e-f41a7746caea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Two speakers overlap</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8711-e13fa01b4547" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Clipped loud segments</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b97c-eeb65602bebb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Silence punctuated by impulses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9bde-f509715687f5" class="">Prediction adversarial set</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9b26-dec132865be1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regime shift mid-series</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-bfcd-d25252f11314" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Missing block</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-99b2-e44664100afe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outlier spike</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-b2f4-c813f7ac2615" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lagged effect (y depends on x(t-3))</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-ab89-d9d3f33e511d" class="">Each adversarial case must produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-b3fd-ff921a99c9cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stress_failures.jsonl</code> if it fails</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-bc38-f6b322091a8d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or metrics showing bounded degradation if it p
asses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-bde7-cd32fd33bb61"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-aa9a-d851981a719f" class="">D. Determinism “hardening” checklist (common hidden gaps)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-9737-db29a5ef51fb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Floating-point</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a885-f7bfd2e93000" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fix dtype: float64 everywhere.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-b557-f9a52c561c6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Round outputs to fixed decimals (e.g., 6) before writing JSON.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-b69f-ed9bbd1076ca" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Hashing</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-bfc7-d396e63d195a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hash raw bytes of canonicalized files (for images: canonical PNG bytes; for audio: canonical WAV PCM bytes; for CSV: normalized line endings + sorted columns if allowed).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-8910-fbdf759ffbfe" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>JSON writing</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-92ad-dd306ea05f16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Use stable key ordering.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8e5e-f5dc9b5537ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No timestamps except in <code>run_header.json</code> labeled <code>logging_only</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" 
ir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-9786-e3443b2516e2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Concurrency</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-8e40-d798070c8771" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Default single-thread. If parallelism exists, enforce deterministic scheduling (usually not worth it for strict mode).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-afe6-f6f534d2e1e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-af34-e4cf9c6b926f" class="">E. Termination logic (close the “what counts as done” gap)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-9774-ff5709f29521" class="">Valid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-9ce6-e0877839dfcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All required outputs present.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-83ae-f405c6ea2471" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">All claims have evidence pointers or explicit <code>Limit</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9ffd-f0485895b5bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DeterminismGate passes (3-run hash match).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-8ea9-e83cae49799c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prediction includes calibrated intervals.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-863b-f14b7194c7f4" class="">Bounded</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-9b73-f759d05aa51f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">One or more declared optional capabilities missing (e.g., ASR), but:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9a85-d597ecd6a33e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Issues recorded as BLOCKER/MAJOR,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8659-dbed1765f2fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">all produced claims remain evidence-linked,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-be26-d6f12402dbe0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">system does not crash and does not speculate.</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a016-df2c65b36ee7" class="">Invalid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-b0bb-d3f1fce9166f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Any of:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-ab8a-ef084693d863" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">non-deterministic outputs,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8184-ca9be21fbcc6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">claims without evidence/Limit,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-9dbc-f8ded0266a46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">prediction emitted without intervals/calibration,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-aa70-d372bbbf4592" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">imports from ARCHIVE,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-8427-c944c14d2a1c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">silent failure (missing dependency but output pretends success).</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-ab8a-e576d82d80b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-8c94-f0a8e536a193" class="">F. Single “close-all-gaps” master prompt for AMOS stress test</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-b02a-eefed4f25519" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ROLE: AMOS Stress Test Kernel (Visual + Sound + Prediction)

MISSION:
Run an offline, deterministic stress test across:
1) Vision (images/frames)
2) Audio (wav)
3) Prediction (time-series)
and produce evidence-linked claims with calibrated uncertainty.

ABSOLUTE RULES:
- Deterministic IDs only: sha256-based.
- Deterministic ordering for all outputs.
- Every claim must link to evidence_ptrs or be typed as Limit.
- Missing capability =&gt; BOUNDED with explicit Issue. Never speculate.
- No network calls. No side effects at import. Python 3.9 compatible.

REQUIRED OUTPUTS (per domain):
stress_header.json, inputs.jsonl, artifacts_manifest.jsonl, findings.json, claims.jsonl,
metrics.json, issues.jsonl, audit_report.json, termination.json

GATES:
- DeterminismGate (3-run hash match)
- EvidenceGate (0 unevidenced claims)
- CalibrationGate (prediction intervals + coverage)
- RobustnessGate (adversarial suite bounded degradation)

TERMINATION:
Valid / Bounded / Invalid with top reasons and blockers.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-9e08-e4ec0373e8e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-94d2-df4881d342d8" class="">If you want the next step in this same thread: I will output the <strong>exact file list + function signatures + pytest plan</strong> for the Stress Test Kernel (including deterministic JSON writer, evidence pointer utilities, and the stage4 regime rules).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-9c11-c747850b1d5b" class="">AMOS Stress Test Spec: Visual + Sound + Prediction (Offline, Deterministic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-920f-cc71ea75aa36" class="">Goal</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8a33-f4b9b1083939" class="">Verify AMOS can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-b36f-cf01b35b887d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Perceive</strong> (image/audio) without hallucinating structure.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-a5d8-e3e125e0d342" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Extract invariants</strong> (objects, text, events, patterns) with evidence traces.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8eab-d295c6213d86" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Predict</strong> (time-series / sequence) with calibrated uncertainty and bounded claims.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-85e0-d77e30a03e91" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Stay deterministic</strong> (same input → same outputs, same IDs, no network).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-bf46-fb62650bfe8d"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-9871-d0222e082edc" class="">1) Stress Test Matrix (What to test)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a06d-d85885ec2f41" class="">A. Visual (Images / Frames)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a988-d5690a53ae7b" class=""><strong>Tasks</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-a1ce-e0a700f82b99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OCR + layout extraction (tables, headings, captions).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-91e6-c9acc4b3d7cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Object/scene attribute extraction (entity list + properties).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-94fa-c8383d0d61e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Change detection (frame t vs t+1).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-85bb-c3066a23bdec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Diagram/flowchart parsing (nodes/edges).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8448-e49cd0407529" class=""><strong>Adversarial cases</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-9e59-f9a26da868dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low-light, blur, partial occlusion, rotated text.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-bb04-c3c46f920e02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Confusable symbols (O/0, l/1, S/5).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a260-e3976e019f19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dense UI screenshots (icons, small fonts).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a547-d4e0c333f8df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Near-duplicate” images with one subtle change.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-8f67-eb1b1e0067bd" class=""><strong>Outputs (must be produced)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-aeea-e6bd2966c996" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>vision_findings.json</code> (entities + evidence pointers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9dbc-e2f9bbf0fdb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>vision_claims.jsonl</code> (UCIA-style support typing per claim)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a1f6-e8c5cd8b4818" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>vision_artifacts/</code> (cropped evidence regions with deterministic IDs)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-bd64-eb88a2cc8e4b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-a9fa-fc1f017d75b3" class="">B. Sound (Audio)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-bceb-ed585e48b145" class=""><strong>Tasks</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a137-e1bea3f43f62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Speech-to-text (if offline model exists; otherwise bounded skip).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-ac11-c1747bafd551" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Speaker segmentation (coarse).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bfb0-d31cc2357bdd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Acoustic event detection (clap, alarm, silence, music onset).</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8612-f5f1c63fef25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Keyword spotting (small deterministic lexicon).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b523-e57de393ed13" class=""><strong>Adversarial cases</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b92c-e20320207b07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Background music + speech</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-a9fd-c953c2071c07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Multiple speakers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bde9-e1d789109184" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Accents + speed changes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bebe-cafa82821ffa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High noise floor / clipping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8886-dd1f2aeb5826" class=""><strong>Outputs</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-a3c7-fc39542cd9c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audio_transcript.jsonl</code> (time-stamped segments, or BOUNDED)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9d9f-f57209152c51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audio_events.jsonl</code> (event spans + confidence)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-952a-e3e9879c325e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>audio_claims.jsonl</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-ba93-dbc9519c7095"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-ab45-d8559db067af" class="">C. Prediction (Time-series / Sequence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-b95b-eb4788e53e32" class=""><strong>Tasks</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9481-d08e122a126a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">1-step and multi-step forecasting.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-a74e-dc0821d560bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regime detection (birth→expansion→dominance→decay stage tagging).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-891a-cde6dc180fcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Anomaly detection.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8c7f-e9d7b849eb22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Counterfactual “what-if” simulation (bounded, model-declared).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-9292-e0bac6a45109" class=""><strong>Adversarial cases</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-91e8-f87b9fb3ad1f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-stationary drift (regime shifts)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9732-c651167247e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Missing data blocks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9248-dd6ca186fe80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outliers + spikes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a48d-c38a9ef7b876" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lagged effects (delayed response)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-82e0-d5281a5f5e67" c
